228	func parseTarget(start *strawpb.RequestStart) (target, *executionError) {
229		if start == nil {
230			return target{}, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
231		}
232	
233		parsed, failure := parseRequestURL(start.GetUrl())
234		if failure != nil {
235			return target{}, failure
236		}
237	
238		port, failure := targetPort(parsed)
239		if failure != nil {
240			return target{}, failure
241		}
242	
243		host := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.ToLower(parsed.Hostname()), ".")
244	
245		return target{host: host, port: port}, nil
246	}
247	
248	func parseRequestURL(raw string) (*url.URL, *executionError) {
249		parsed, err := url.Parse(raw)
250		if err != nil || parsed.Hostname() == "" {
251			return nil, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
252		}
253	
254		return parsed, nil
255	}

257	func targetPort(parsed *url.URL) (uint32, *executionError) {
258		port := parsed.Port()
259	
260		if port == "" {
261			port = defaultPort(parsed.Scheme)
262		}
263	
264		if port == "" {
265			return 0, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
266		}
267	
268		n, err := strconv.ParseUint(port, 10, 32)
269		if err != nil || n == 0 || n > 65535 {
270			return 0, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
271		}
272	
273		return uint32(n), nil
274	}

276	func defaultPort(scheme string) string {
277		switch scheme {
278		case "http":
279			return defaultHTTPPort
280		case "https":
281			return defaultHTTPSPort
282		default:
283			return ""
284		}
285	}

287	func validateStart(start *strawpb.RequestStart) *executionError {
288		err := start.Validate()
289		if err != nil {
290			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
291		}
292	
293		if start.GetMode() != strawpb.RequestMode_REQUEST_MODE_DECODED_HTTP {
294			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
295		}
296	
297		if start.GetRedirectPolicy() != strawpb.RedirectPolicy_REDIRECT_POLICY_NO_FOLLOW {
298			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
299		}
300	
301		if start.GetDestinationPolicy().GetResolutionMode() != strawpb.DestinationResolutionMode_DESTINATION_RESOLUTION_DIRECT_LOCAL {
302			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED, unsupportedModeFact)
303		}
304	
305		if start.GetFingerprintInstruction() != "" {
306			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_UNSUPPORTED_FINGERPRINT, unsupportedFingerprint)
307		}
308	
309		if strings.EqualFold(start.GetMethod(), http.MethodConnect) {
310			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
311		}
312	
313		return nil
314	}
315	
316	func buildHTTPRequest(ctx context.Context, start *strawpb.RequestStart, body []byte) (*http.Request, *executionError) {
317		var reader io.Reader
318		if body != nil {
319			reader = bytes.NewReader(body)
320		}
321	
322		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, start.GetMethod(), start.GetUrl(), reader)
323		if err != nil {
324			return nil, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidRequestStartFact)
325		}
326	
327		for _, h := range start.GetHeaders() {
328			if !safeOutboundHeader(h.GetName(), h.GetValue()) {
329				return nil, executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, injectionFactFailed)
330			}
331	
332			req.Header.Add(h.GetName(), string(h.GetValue()))
333		}
334	
335		failure := applyInjection(req.Header, start.GetInjectionOperations())
336		if failure != nil {
337			return nil, failure
338		}
339	
340		return req, nil
341	}
342	
343	func applyInjection(headers http.Header, ops []*strawpb.InjectionOperation) *executionError {
344		seenSet := map[string]struct{}{}
345	
346		for _, op := range ops {
347			name := op.GetHeaderName()
348			if !safeOutboundHeader(name, op.GetValue()) {
349				return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, injectionFactFailed)
350			}
351	
352			key := strings.ToLower(name)
353	
354			switch op.GetOp() {
355			case opSet:
356				if _, ok := seenSet[key]; ok {
357					return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, injectionFactFailed)
358				}
359	
360				seenSet[key] = struct{}{}
361	
362				headers.Set(name, string(op.GetValue()))
363			case opAppend:
364				headers.Add(name, string(op.GetValue()))
365			case opRemove:
366				headers.Del(name)
367			default:
368				return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, injectionFactFailed)
369			}
370		}
371	
372		return nil
373	}
374	
375	func safeOutboundHeader(name string, value []byte) bool {
376		if !validHTTPToken(name) {
377			return false
378		}
379	
380		if deniedHeader(name) {
381			return false
382		}
383	
384		return !bytes.ContainsAny(value, "\r\n")
385	}
386	
387	func deniedHeader(name string) bool {
388		switch strings.ToLower(name) {
389		case "host", "content-length", "transfer-encoding", "connection", "proxy-authorization":
390			return true
391		default:
392			return strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(name), "x-straw-")
393		}
394	}
395	
396	func validHTTPToken(s string) bool {
397		if s == "" {
398			return false
399		}
400	
401		for _, r := range s {
402			idx := int(r)
403			if idx > 255 || !httpTokenAllowed[idx] {
404				return false
405			}
406		}
407	
408		return true
409	}
410	
411	var httpTokenAllowed = func() [256]bool {
412		var allowed [256]bool
413	
414		for _, r := range "!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~" {
415			allowed[byte(r)] = true
416		}
417	
418		for r := byte('0'); r <= '9'; r++ {
419			allowed[r] = true
420		}
421	
422		for r := byte('A'); r <= 'Z'; r++ {
423			allowed[r] = true
424		}
425	
426		for r := byte('a'); r <= 'z'; r++ {
427			allowed[r] = true
428		}
429	
430		return allowed
431	}()
432	
433	func responseHeaders(headers http.Header) []*strawpb.Header {
434		names := make([]string, 0, len(headers))
435		for name := range headers {
436			names = append(names, name)
437		}
438	
439		sort.Strings(names)
440	
441		out := make([]*strawpb.Header, 0, len(headers))
442		for _, name := range names {
443			for _, value := range headers.Values(name) {
444				out = append(out, &strawpb.Header{Name: name, Value: []byte(value)})
445			}
446		}
447	
448		return out
449	}

451	type executionError struct {
452		code        strawpb.ErrorCode
453		fact        string
454		timeoutType strawpb.TimeoutType
455	}
456	
457	func (e *executionError) Error() string {
458		return fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s", e.code, e.fact)
459	}
460	
461	func executorFailure(code strawpb.ErrorCode, fact string) *executionError {
462		return &executionError{code: code, fact: fact}
463	}
464	
465	func timeoutFailure() *executionError {
466		return &executionError{
467			code:        strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_TIMEOUT_EXCEEDED,
468			fact:        deadlineExceededFact,
469			timeoutType: strawpb.TimeoutType_TIMEOUT_TYPE_TOTAL_DEADLINE_TIMEOUT,
470		}
471	}
472	
473	func mapHTTPError(ctx context.Context, err error) *executionError {
474		var failure *executionError
475		if errors.As(err, &failure) {
476			return failure
477		}
478	
479		if errors.Is(ctx.Err(), context.DeadlineExceeded) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
480			return timeoutFailure()
481		}
482	
483		if errors.Is(err, syscall.ECONNREFUSED) {
484			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_UPSTREAM_CONNECTION_REFUSED, tcpRefusedFact)
485		}
486	
487		if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) || errors.Is(err, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF) {
488			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_UPSTREAM_RESET, upstreamResetFact)
489		}
490	
491		if looksLikeTLSError(err) {
492			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_UPSTREAM_TLS_FAILURE, tlsHandshakeFailedFact)
493		}
494	
495		return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, executorInternalFact)
496	}
497	
498	func looksLikeTLSError(err error) bool {
499		s := strings.ToLower(err.Error())
500	
501		return strings.Contains(s, "tls") || strings.Contains(s, "certificate")
502	}
503	
504	func netIPAddr(ip net.IPAddr) (netip.Addr, bool) {
505		if v4 := ip.IP.To4(); v4 != nil {
506			addr, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(v4)
507	
508			return addr, ok
509		}
510	
511		addr, ok := netip.AddrFromSlice(ip.IP)
512	
513		return addr, ok
514	}
515	
516	func validateResolvedIP(policy *strawpb.DestinationPolicy, addr netip.Addr) *executionError {
517		if policy == nil {
518			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_EXECUTOR_INTERNAL_ERROR, invalidDestinationFact)
519		}
520	
521		if addr.Is4In6() {
522			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED, dnsDeniedIPFact)
523		}
524	
525		failure := validateCIDRPolicy(policy, addr)
526		if failure != nil {
527			return failure
528		}
529	
530		if deniedByDefault(policy, addr) {
531			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED, dnsDeniedIPFact)
532		}
533	
534		return nil
535	}
536	
537	func validateCIDRPolicy(policy *strawpb.DestinationPolicy, addr netip.Addr) *executionError {
538		allowed, failure := parsePrefixes(policy.GetAllowedCidrs())
539		if failure != nil {
540			return failure
541		}
542	
543		if len(allowed) > 0 && !prefixesContain(allowed, addr) {
544			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED, dnsDeniedIPFact)
545		}
546	
547		denied, failure := parsePrefixes(policy.GetDeniedCidrs())
548		if failure != nil {
549			return failure
550		}
551	
552		if prefixesContain(denied, addr) {
553			return executorFailure(strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED, dnsDeniedIPFact)
554		}
555	
556		return nil
557	}
558	
559	func deniedByDefault(policy *strawpb.DestinationPolicy, addr netip.Addr) bool {
560		if blockedUnless(policy.GetAllowMetadataIps(), isMetadataIP(addr)) {
561			return true
562		}
563	
564		if blockedUnless(policy.GetAllowLoopback(), addr.IsLoopback()) {
565			return true
566		}
567	
568		if blockedUnless(policy.GetAllowLinkLocal(), addr.IsLinkLocalUnicast()) {
569			return true
570		}
571	
572		if blockedUnless(policy.GetAllowMulticast(), addr.IsMulticast()) {
573			return true
574		}
575	
576		if blockedUnless(policy.GetAllowPrivateRanges(), addr.IsPrivate()) {
577			return true
578		}
579	
580		return prefixesContain(defaultDeniedPrefixes, addr)
581	}

1	// Package natsx contains NATS helpers for Straw services.
2	package natsx
3	
4	import (

8		"unicode"
9	)
10	
11	const payloadSafetyMargin = 64 * 1024
12	
13	var (
14		errUnsupportedStreamDirection = errors.New("unsupported stream direction")
15		errMaxPayloadTooSmall         = errors.New("nats max payload must exceed safety margin")
16		errFrameBodyLimitTooLarge     = errors.New("configured frame/body limit exceeds nats max payload")
17		errNATSServersRequired        = errors.New("nats servers are required")
18		errNATSServerEmpty            = errors.New("nats server is empty")
19		errSubjectTokenRequired       = errors.New("subject token is required")
20		errSubjectTokenUnsafe         = errors.New("subject token contains unsafe character")
21	)
22	
23	// StreamDirection identifies the direction of a stream subject.
24	type StreamDirection string
25	
26	const (
27		// DirectionControlToExecutor marks control-to-executor stream subjects.
28		DirectionControlToExecutor StreamDirection = "c2e"
29		// DirectionExecutorToControl marks executor-to-control stream subjects.
30		DirectionExecutorToControl StreamDirection = "e2c"
31	)
32	
33	// RegistrationSubject returns the NATS subject for worker registration.
34	func RegistrationSubject() string {
35		return "straw.v1.control.register"
36	}
37	
38	// HeartbeatSubject returns the NATS subject for worker heartbeats.
39	func HeartbeatSubject() string {
40		return "straw.v1.control.heartbeat"
41	}
42	
43	// ControlInboxPrefix returns the reply-inbox prefix used by control clients.
44	func ControlInboxPrefix() string {
45		return "_INBOX.ctl"
46	}
47	
48	// WorkerInboxPrefix returns the worker-specific reply inbox prefix.
49	func WorkerInboxPrefix(workerID string) (string, error) {
50		err := validateSubjectToken(workerID)
51		if err != nil {
52			return "", fmt.Errorf("worker_id: %w", err)
53		}
54	
55		return "_INBOX.wrk." + workerID, nil
56	}
57	
58	// ValidateSubjectToken reports whether token is a safe NATS subject token.
59	func ValidateSubjectToken(token string) error {
60		return validateSubjectToken(token)
61	}
62	
63	// AssignmentSubject returns the assignment subject for a worker session.
64	func AssignmentSubject(workerID, sessionID string) (string, error) {
65		err := validateSubjectToken(workerID)
66		if err != nil {
67			return "", fmt.Errorf("worker_id: %w", err)
68		}
69	
70		err = validateSubjectToken(sessionID)
71		if err != nil {
72			return "", fmt.Errorf("session_id: %w", err)
73		}
74	
75		return fmt.Sprintf("straw.v1.executor.%s.%s.assign", workerID, sessionID), nil
76	}
77	
78	// StreamSubject returns the stream subject for a request, worker, and session.
79	func StreamSubject(requestID, workerID, sessionID string, direction StreamDirection) (string, error) {
80		err := validateSubjectToken(requestID)
81		if err != nil {
82			return "", fmt.Errorf("request_id: %w", err)
83		}
84	
85		err = validateSubjectToken(workerID)
86		if err != nil {
87			return "", fmt.Errorf("worker_id: %w", err)
88		}
89	
90		err = validateSubjectToken(sessionID)
91		if err != nil {
92			return "", fmt.Errorf("session_id: %w", err)
93		}
94	
95		switch direction {
96		case DirectionControlToExecutor, DirectionExecutorToControl:
97			return fmt.Sprintf("straw.v1.req.%s.%s.%s.%s", requestID, workerID, sessionID, direction), nil
98		default:
99			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", errUnsupportedStreamDirection, direction)
100		}
101	}
102	
103	// TerminalSubject returns the terminal subject for a request stream.
104	func TerminalSubject(requestID, workerID, sessionID string, direction StreamDirection) (string, error) {
105		return StreamSubject(requestID, workerID, sessionID, direction)
106	}
107	
108	// ValidateMaxPayload checks the configured NATS payload limits.
109	func ValidateMaxPayload(maxPayloadBytes *uint64, maxFrameDataBytes, maxInlineRequestBodyBytes, maxInlineResponseBodyBytes uint64) error {
110		if maxPayloadBytes == nil {
111			return nil
112		}
113	
114		if *maxPayloadBytes <= payloadSafetyMargin {
115			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %d <= %d", errMaxPayloadTooSmall, *maxPayloadBytes, payloadSafetyMargin)
116		}
117	
118		limit := max(maxInlineRequestBodyBytes, maxFrameDataBytes)
119	
120		limit = max(limit, maxInlineResponseBodyBytes)
121	
122		if limit > *maxPayloadBytes-payloadSafetyMargin {
123			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %d > %d - %d safety margin", errFrameBodyLimitTooLarge, limit, *maxPayloadBytes, payloadSafetyMargin)
124		}
125	
126		return nil
127	}
128	
129	// ValidateServers validates the configured NATS server list.
130	func ValidateServers(servers []string) error {
131		if len(servers) == 0 {
132			return errNATSServersRequired
133		}
134	
135		for i, server := range servers {
136			if strings.TrimSpace(server) == "" {
137				return fmt.Errorf("%w: %d", errNATSServerEmpty, i)
138			}
139		}
140	
141		return nil
142	}
143	
144	func validateSubjectToken(token string) error {
145		if token == "" {
146			return errSubjectTokenRequired
147		}
148	
149		for _, r := range token {
150			switch {
151			case unicode.IsLetter(r), unicode.IsDigit(r), r == '-', r == '_':
152			default:
153				return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", errSubjectTokenUnsafe, r)
154			}
155		}
156	
157		return nil
158	}

1	package natsx
2	
3	import (
4		"time"
5	
6		strawpb "github.com/beremaran/straw/v2/api/proto/straw/v1"
7	)
8	
9	// FrameOutcome is the result of validating one StreamFrame against a
10	// per-direction, per-attempt stream (docs/planning/12 "Stream Ordering and
11	// Sequencing"). Ignored/counted outcomes contribute to worker cooldown; the
12	// caller is responsible for that accounting.
13	type FrameOutcome int
14	
15	const (
16		// FrameAccepted means the frame was the next expected frame and was
17		// applied to stream state.
18		FrameAccepted FrameOutcome = iota
19		// FrameDuplicate means stream_seq was lower than expected: ignored and
20		// counted.
21		FrameDuplicate
22		// FrameAfterTerminal means a frame arrived after a terminal frame:
23		// ignored and counted.
24		FrameAfterTerminal
25		// FrameSequenceGap means stream_seq was higher than expected: a protocol
26		// error.
27		FrameSequenceGap
28		// FrameOffsetMismatch means a DataFrame offset did not equal the
29		// cumulative bytes previously accepted: a protocol error.
30		FrameOffsetMismatch
31		// FrameCreditExhausted means a DataFrame carried more bytes than the
32		// remaining granted credit: a protocol error.
33		FrameCreditExhausted
34		// FrameAttemptMismatch means the frame attempt did not equal the active
35		// attempt: a protocol error.
36		FrameAttemptMismatch
37		// FrameInvalid means the frame was nil or carried no payload.
38		FrameInvalid
39	)
40	
41	// IsProtocolError reports whether the outcome is a hard protocol violation
42	// (as opposed to an ignored/counted duplicate or late frame).
43	func (o FrameOutcome) IsProtocolError() bool {
44		return o == FrameSequenceGap || o == FrameOffsetMismatch || o == FrameCreditExhausted || o == FrameAttemptMismatch || o == FrameInvalid
45	}
46	
47	// StreamValidator enforces the ordering, offset, credit, and terminal rules
48	// for a single stream direction and attempt (docs/planning/12). A validator
49	// models the receiving side of one byte stream: it holds the data-byte credit
50	// it has granted to the sender and rejects DataFrames that exceed it.
51	//
52	// stream_seq starts at 1 for each (attempt, direction). Credit applies only to
53	// DataFrame payload bytes; control and terminal frames never consume credit.
54	type StreamValidator struct {
55		attempt      uint32
56		expectedSeq  uint64
57		offset       uint64
58		credit       uint64
59		terminal     bool
60		idleTimeout  time.Duration
61		lastActivity time.Time
62		now          func() time.Time
63	}
64	
65	// NewStreamValidator builds a validator for the given active attempt.
66	// initialCredit is the data-byte credit initially granted to the sender
67	// (AssignRequest initial upload/download credit). idleTimeout of zero disables
68	// the idle check. now may be nil (defaults to time.Now).
69	func NewStreamValidator(attempt uint32, initialCredit uint64, idleTimeout time.Duration, now func() time.Time) *StreamValidator {
70		if now == nil {
71			now = time.Now
72		}
73	
74		return &StreamValidator{
75			attempt:      attempt,
76			expectedSeq:  1,
77			credit:       initialCredit,
78			idleTimeout:  idleTimeout,
79			lastActivity: now(),
80			now:          now,
81		}
82	}
83	
84	// GrantCredit adds data-byte credit for the sender (the receiver calls this
85	// when it emits a CreditFrame on the reverse channel after releasing buffered
86	// bytes).
87	func (v *StreamValidator) GrantCredit(bytes uint64) {
88		v.credit += bytes
89	}
90	
91	// Terminated reports whether a terminal frame has been accepted.
92	func (v *StreamValidator) Terminated() bool { return v.terminal }
93	
94	// ExpectedSeq returns the next stream_seq the validator will accept.
95	func (v *StreamValidator) ExpectedSeq() uint64 { return v.expectedSeq }
96	
97	// RemainingCredit returns the data-byte credit still available to the sender.
98	func (v *StreamValidator) RemainingCredit() uint64 { return v.credit }
99	
100	// Accept validates f and, on FrameAccepted, advances stream state. All other
101	// outcomes leave state unchanged.
102	func (v *StreamValidator) Accept(f *strawpb.StreamFrame) FrameOutcome {
103		if outcome := v.validateFrameShell(f); outcome != FrameAccepted {
104			return outcome
105		}
106	
107		seq := f.GetStreamSeq()
108		switch {
109		case seq < v.expectedSeq:
110			return FrameDuplicate
111		case seq > v.expectedSeq:
112			return FrameSequenceGap
113		}
114	
115		// stream_seq is exactly the next expected value. Apply payload-specific
116		// validation before committing.
117		if outcome := validateP0Payload(f); outcome != FrameAccepted {
118			return outcome
119		}
120	
121		if outcome := v.acceptData(f.GetData()); outcome != FrameAccepted {
122			return outcome
123		}
124	
125		v.expectedSeq++
126	
127		v.lastActivity = v.now()
128		if isTerminalFrame(f) {
129			v.terminal = true
130		}
131	
132		return FrameAccepted
133	}
134	
135	// IdleExpired reports whether the frame idle timeout has elapsed since the
136	// last accepted frame. It is never true once the stream is terminated.
137	func (v *StreamValidator) IdleExpired() bool {
138		if v.idleTimeout <= 0 || v.terminal {
139			return false
140		}
141	
142		return v.now().Sub(v.lastActivity) >= v.idleTimeout
143	}
144	
145	func (v *StreamValidator) validateFrameShell(f *strawpb.StreamFrame) FrameOutcome {
146		if f == nil || f.GetPayload() == nil {
147			return FrameInvalid
148		}
149	
150		if v.terminal {
151			return FrameAfterTerminal
152		}
153	
154		if f.GetStreamSeq() == 0 {
155			return FrameInvalid
156		}
157	
158		if f.GetAttempt() != v.attempt {
159			return FrameAttemptMismatch
160		}
161	
162		return FrameAccepted
163	}
164	
165	func validateP0Payload(f *strawpb.StreamFrame) FrameOutcome {
166		if _, ok := f.GetPayload().(*strawpb.StreamFrame_BodyRef); ok {
167			return FrameInvalid
168		}
169	
170		return FrameAccepted
171	}
172	
173	func (v *StreamValidator) acceptData(data *strawpb.DataFrame) FrameOutcome {
174		if data == nil {
175			return FrameAccepted
176		}
177	
178		if data.GetOffset() != v.offset {
179			return FrameOffsetMismatch
180		}
181	
182		n := uint64(len(data.GetData()))
183		if n > v.credit {
184			return FrameCreditExhausted
185		}
186	
187		v.credit -= n
188		v.offset += n
189	
190		return FrameAccepted
191	}
192	
193	// isTerminalFrame reports whether f carries a terminal payload (EndFrame,
194	// ErrorFrame, or CancelledFrame) per docs/planning/09 "Terminal Rule".
195	func isTerminalFrame(f *strawpb.StreamFrame) bool {
196		switch f.GetPayload().(type) {
197		case *strawpb.StreamFrame_End, *strawpb.StreamFrame_Error, *strawpb.StreamFrame_Cancelled:
198			return true
199		default:
200			return false
201		}
202	}

12	// Connection wraps a live NATS connection for Straw services.
13	type Connection struct {
14		*nats.Conn
15	}
16	
17	const (
18		defaultReconnectWait = 2 * time.Second
19		defaultPingInterval  = 30 * time.Second
20	)
21	
22	var (
23		errNATSConnectionRequired = errors.New("nats connection is required")
24		errNATSMaxPayloadNegative = errors.New("nats max payload must be non-negative")
25	)

27	// ConnectOptions configures a Straw NATS client connection.
28	type ConnectOptions struct {
29		Servers             []string
30		UserCredentialsFile string
31		ReconnectAttempts   int
32		ReconnectWait       time.Duration
33		PingInterval        time.Duration
34		MaxPingFailures     int
35	}
36	
37	// Connect dials the configured NATS servers and returns a live connection.
38	func Connect(opts ConnectOptions) (*Connection, error) {
39		err := ValidateServers(opts.Servers)
40		if err != nil {
41			return nil, err
42		}
43	
44		if opts.ReconnectAttempts <= 0 {
45			opts.ReconnectAttempts = 10
46		}
47	
48		if opts.ReconnectWait <= 0 {
49			opts.ReconnectWait = defaultReconnectWait
50		}
51	
52		if opts.PingInterval <= 0 {
53			opts.PingInterval = defaultPingInterval
54		}
55	
56		if opts.MaxPingFailures <= 0 {
57			opts.MaxPingFailures = 3
58		}
59	
60		natsOpts := []nats.Option{
61			nats.DontRandomize(),
62			nats.MaxReconnects(opts.ReconnectAttempts),
63			nats.ReconnectWait(opts.ReconnectWait),
64			nats.PingInterval(opts.PingInterval),
65			nats.MaxPingsOutstanding(opts.MaxPingFailures),
66		}
67	
68		if opts.UserCredentialsFile != "" {
69			natsOpts = append(natsOpts, nats.UserCredentials(opts.UserCredentialsFile))
70		}
71	
72		conn, err := nats.Connect(joinServers(opts.Servers), natsOpts...)
73		if err != nil {
74			return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect nats: %w", err)
75		}
76	
77		return &Connection{Conn: conn}, nil
78	}
79	
80	// ValidateConnectedMaxPayload checks the live server-advertised payload limit
81	// against Straw's configured frame and body limits.
82	func ValidateConnectedMaxPayload(conn maxPayloadProvider, maxFrameDataBytes, maxInlineRequestBodyBytes, maxInlineResponseBodyBytes uint64) error {
83		if conn == nil {
84			return errNATSConnectionRequired
85		}
86	
87		maxPayload := conn.MaxPayload()
88		if maxPayload < 0 {
89			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %d", errNATSMaxPayloadNegative, maxPayload)
90		}
91	
92		maxPayloadBytes := uint64(maxPayload)
93	
94		return ValidateMaxPayload(&maxPayloadBytes, maxFrameDataBytes, maxInlineRequestBodyBytes, maxInlineResponseBodyBytes)
95	}
96	
97	type maxPayloadProvider interface {
98		MaxPayload() int64
99	}
100	
101	func joinServers(servers []string) string {
102		return strings.Join(servers, ",")
103	}

1	package control

3	import (
4		"context"

7	
8		"github.com/nats-io/nats.go"
9	
10		strawpb "github.com/beremaran/straw/v2/api/proto/straw/v1"

12	)
13	
14	const workerNATSInvalidMessage = "invalid_nats_message"
15	
16	var (
17		errSetupConnRequired = errors.New("nats connection is required")
18		errSetupRegRequired  = errors.New("worker registry is required")
19	)
20	
21	// SetupWorkerDiscoverySubscriptions wires the live NATS worker discovery
22	// subjects into the registry and flushes the subscriptions before returning.
23	func SetupWorkerDiscoverySubscriptions(conn *natsx.Connection, reg *WorkerRegistry) error {
24		if conn == nil {
25			return fmt.Errorf("setup worker discovery: %w", errSetupConnRequired)
26		}
27	
28		if reg == nil {
29			return fmt.Errorf("setup worker discovery: %w", errSetupRegRequired)
30		}
31	
32		var err error
33	
34		_, err = conn.QueueSubscribe(natsx.RegistrationSubject(), "control", func(msg *nats.Msg) {
35			replyWorkerRegister(reg, msg)
36		})
37		if err != nil {
38			return fmt.Errorf("subscribe register: %w", err)
39		}
40	
41		_, err = conn.QueueSubscribe(natsx.HeartbeatSubject(), "control", func(msg *nats.Msg) {
42			replyWorkerHeartbeat(reg, msg)
43		})
44		if err != nil {
45			return fmt.Errorf("subscribe heartbeat: %w", err)
46		}
47	
48		err = conn.Flush()
49		if err != nil {
50			return fmt.Errorf("flush worker discovery subscriptions: %w", err)
51		}
52	
53		return nil
54	}
55	
56	func replyWorkerRegister(reg *WorkerRegistry, msg *nats.Msg) {
57		ack := &strawpb.RegisterAck{Ok: false, Error: workerNATSInvalidMessage}
58	
59		env, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(msg.Data)
60		if err == nil {
61			handleRegister(reg, ack, env)
62		}
63	
64		reply := buildReplyEnvelope(env, &strawpb.Envelope{
65			ProtocolMajor: ProtocolMajor,
66			ProtocolMinor: 0,
67			Payload:       &strawpb.Envelope_RegisterAck{RegisterAck: ack},
68		})
69	
70		replyEnvelope(msg, reply)
71	}
72	
73	func handleRegister(reg *WorkerRegistry, ack *strawpb.RegisterAck, env *strawpb.Envelope) {
74		req := env.GetRegisterRequest()
75		if req == nil {
76			return
77		}
78	
79		outcome, regErr := reg.Register(context.Background(), req)
80		if regErr != nil {
81			ack.Error = errorCodeControlInternalError
82	
83			return
84		}
85	
86		if outcome.OK {
87			ack.Ok = true
88			ack.SessionId = outcome.SessionID
89			ack.Error = ""
90		} else {
91			ack.Error = outcome.Reason
92		}
93	}
94	
95	func replyWorkerHeartbeat(reg *WorkerRegistry, msg *nats.Msg) {
96		ack := &strawpb.HeartbeatAck{Ok: false, Error: workerNATSInvalidMessage}
97	
98		env, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(msg.Data)
99		if err == nil {
100			handleHeartbeat(reg, ack, env)
101		}
102	
103		reply := buildReplyEnvelope(env, &strawpb.Envelope{
104			ProtocolMajor: ProtocolMajor,
105			ProtocolMinor: 0,
106			Payload:       &strawpb.Envelope_HeartbeatAck{HeartbeatAck: ack},
107		})
108	
109		replyEnvelope(msg, reply)
110	}
111	
112	func handleHeartbeat(reg *WorkerRegistry, ack *strawpb.HeartbeatAck, env *strawpb.Envelope) {
113		req := env.GetHeartbeatRequest()
114		if req == nil {
115			return
116		}
117	
118		ok, regErr := reg.Heartbeat(req)
119		if regErr != nil {
120			ack.Error = errorCodeControlInternalError
121	
122			return
123		}
124	
125		if ok {
126			ack.Ok = true
127			ack.Error = ""
128		} else {
129			ack.Error = "unknown_worker_session"
130		}
131	}
132	
133	func buildReplyEnvelope(env *strawpb.Envelope, reply *strawpb.Envelope) *strawpb.Envelope {
134		if env == nil {
135			return reply
136		}
137	
138		reply.RequestId = env.GetRequestId()
139		reply.TenantId = env.GetTenantId()
140		reply.TraceId = env.GetTraceId()
141		reply.DeadlineUnixMs = env.GetDeadlineUnixMs()
142		reply.Attempt = env.GetAttempt()
143	
144		reply.TraceContext = append([]byte(nil), env.GetTraceContext()...)
145		if env.GetProtocolMinor() != 0 {
146			reply.ProtocolMinor = env.GetProtocolMinor()
147		}
148	
149		return reply
150	}
151	
152	func replyEnvelope(msg *nats.Msg, env *strawpb.Envelope) {
153		var err error
154	
155		raw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(env)
156		if err != nil {
157			return
158		}
159	
160		err = msg.Respond(raw)
161		if err != nil {
162			return
163		}
164	}

1	package natsx
2	
3	import (
4		"errors"
5		"testing"
6	)
7	
8	var errNoReadySubscriber = errors.New("no ready subscriber")
9	
10	type orderingHarness struct {
11		ready map[string]bool
12		seen  map[string]int
13	}
14	
15	func newOrderingHarness() *orderingHarness {
16		return &orderingHarness{
17			ready: make(map[string]bool),
18			seen:  make(map[string]int),
19		}
20	}
21	
22	func (h *orderingHarness) subscribe(subject string) {
23		h.ready[subject] = false
24	}
25	
26	func (h *orderingHarness) flush(subject string) {
27		h.ready[subject] = true
28	}
29	
30	func (h *orderingHarness) publish(subject string) error {
31		if !h.ready[subject] {
32			return errNoReadySubscriber
33		}
34	
35		h.seen[subject]++
36	
37		return nil
38	}
39	
40	func TestNATSOrderingRequiresFlushedStreamSubscription(t *testing.T) {
41		t.Parallel()
42	
43		h := newOrderingHarness()
44		subject, err := StreamSubject("req_1", "worker_1", "sess_1", DirectionControlToExecutor)
45		if err != nil {
46			t.Fatalf("StreamSubject() error = %v", err)
47		}
48	
49		err = h.publish(subject)
50		if !errors.Is(err, errNoReadySubscriber) {
51			t.Fatalf("publish before subscribe error = %v, want errNoReadySubscriber", err)
52		}
53	
54		h.subscribe(subject)
55		err = h.publish(subject)
56		if !errors.Is(err, errNoReadySubscriber) {
57			t.Fatalf("publish before flush error = %v, want errNoReadySubscriber", err)
58		}
59	
60		h.flush(subject)
61		err = h.publish(subject)
62		if err != nil {
63			t.Fatalf("publish after flush error = %v", err)
64		}
65		if h.seen[subject] != 1 {
66			t.Fatalf("delivered count = %d, want 1", h.seen[subject])
67		}
68	}

1	package egress

9	)
10	
11	// ProtocolMajor is the worker protocol major version this worker speaks. It
12	// must match the Control-side constant for registration to succeed.
13	const ProtocolMajor uint32 = 1
14	
15	// Identity holds the stable identity a worker registers with.
16	type Identity struct {
17		WorkerID     string
18		CredentialID string
19		ExecutorType string
20		PrivateKey   ed25519.PrivateKey
21	}
22	
23	// Capabilities are the capability claims a worker advertises at registration.
24	// They must be within the worker credential's allowed scope or Control rejects
25	// the registration.
26	type Capabilities struct {
27		AllowedPools          []strawpb.RegisterRequest_PoolRef
28		Tags                  []string
29		Countries             []string
30		Regions               []string
31		IPTypes               []string
32		SupportedIngressModes []string
33		MaxConcurrency        uint32
34		SoftwareVersion       string
35		InitialDraining       bool
36	}
37	
38	// BuildRegisterRequest assembles and signs a RegisterRequest for the worker.
39	// The returned message carries the ed25519 signature over its canonical
40	// payload in SignedToken.
41	func BuildRegisterRequest(id Identity, caps Capabilities) *strawpb.RegisterRequest {
42		pools := make([]*strawpb.RegisterRequest_PoolRef, 0, len(caps.AllowedPools))
43		for i := range caps.AllowedPools {
44			p := caps.AllowedPools[i]
45			pools = append(pools, &strawpb.RegisterRequest_PoolRef{TenantId: p.TenantId, PoolId: p.PoolId})
46		}
47	
48		req := &strawpb.RegisterRequest{
49			WorkerId:              id.WorkerID,
50			ExecutorType:          id.ExecutorType,
51			CredentialId:          id.CredentialID,
52			ProtocolMajor:         ProtocolMajor,
53			ProtocolMinor:         0,
54			SoftwareVersion:       caps.SoftwareVersion,
55			AllowedPools:          pools,
56			Tags:                  caps.Tags,
57			Countries:             caps.Countries,
58			Regions:               caps.Regions,
59			IpTypes:               caps.IPTypes,
60			SupportedIngressModes: caps.SupportedIngressModes,
61			MaxConcurrency:        caps.MaxConcurrency,
62			InitialDraining:       caps.InitialDraining,
63		}
64		req.SignedToken = strawpb.SignRegistration(id.PrivateKey, req)
65	
66		return req
67	}
68	
69	// InboxPrefix returns the scoped reply-inbox prefix this worker must configure
70	// on its NATS request/reply client (`_INBOX.wrk.<worker_id>`), per the ACL
71	// table in docs/planning/12-nats-protocol.md.
72	func (id Identity) InboxPrefix() (string, error) {
73		prefix, err := natsx.WorkerInboxPrefix(id.WorkerID)
74		if err != nil {
75			return "", fmt.Errorf("worker inbox prefix: %w", err)
76		}
77	
78		return prefix, nil
79	}
80	
81	// BuildHeartbeat assembles a HeartbeatRequest for the given active session.
82	func BuildHeartbeat(id Identity, sessionID string, health strawpb.WorkerHealth, activeRequests, availableCapacity, maxConcurrency uint32, draining bool) *strawpb.HeartbeatRequest {
83		return &strawpb.HeartbeatRequest{
84			WorkerId:          id.WorkerID,
85			SessionId:         sessionID,
86			Health:            health,
87			ActiveRequests:    activeRequests,
88			AvailableCapacity: availableCapacity,
89			MaxConcurrency:    maxConcurrency,
90			Draining:          draining,
91		}
92	}

17			t.Fatalf("mode decision = %v, want rejected_unsupported", got)
18		}

21		}

130	type fakeNATSServer struct {
131		ln         net.Listener
132		maxPayload int
133	
134		mu     sync.Mutex
135		active net.Conn
136	}
137	
138	func newFakeNATSServer(t *testing.T, maxPayload int) *fakeNATSServer {
139		t.Helper()
140	
141		lc := net.ListenConfig{}
142		ln, err := lc.Listen(context.Background(), "tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
143		if err != nil {
144			t.Fatalf("net.Listen() error = %v", err)
145		}
146	
147		srv := &fakeNATSServer{ln: ln, maxPayload: maxPayload}
148		go srv.acceptLoop()
149		t.Cleanup(func() {
150			srv.Close()
151		})
152	
153		return srv
154	}
155	
156	func (s *fakeNATSServer) URL() string {
157		return "nats://" + s.ln.Addr().String()
158	}
159	
160	func (s *fakeNATSServer) Close() {
161		_ = s.ln.Close()
162		s.CloseActive()
163	}
164	
165	func (s *fakeNATSServer) CloseActive() {
166		s.mu.Lock()
167		active := s.active
168		s.mu.Unlock()
169	
170		if active != nil {
171			_ = active.Close()
172		}
173	}
174	
175	func (s *fakeNATSServer) acceptLoop() {
176		for {
177			conn, err := s.ln.Accept()
178			if err != nil {
179				return
180			}
181	
182			s.mu.Lock()
183			s.active = conn
184			s.mu.Unlock()
185	
186			go s.serveConn(conn)
187		}
188	}
189	
190	func (s *fakeNATSServer) serveConn(conn net.Conn) {
191		defer s.clearActive(conn)
192	
193		info := struct {
194			ServerID    string   `json:"server_id"`
195			Version     string   `json:"version"`
196			Proto       int      `json:"proto"`
197			MaxPayload  int      `json:"max_payload"`
198			ConnectURLs []string `json:"connect_urls,omitempty"`
199		}{
200			ServerID:   "straw-test",
201			Version:    "1.0.0",
202			Proto:      1,
203			MaxPayload: s.maxPayload,
204		}
205	
206		raw, err := json.Marshal(info)
207		if err != nil {
208			_ = conn.Close()
209	
210			return
211		}
212	
213		_, err = fmt.Fprintf(conn, "INFO %s\r\n", raw)
214		if err != nil {
215			_ = conn.Close()
216	
217			return
218		}
219	
220		reader := bufio.NewReader(conn)
221		for {
222			line, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
223			if err != nil {
224				return
225			}
226	
227			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
228			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "PING") {
229				_, err = conn.Write([]byte("PONG\r\n"))
230				if err != nil {
231					return
232				}
233			}
234		}
235	}
236	
237	func (s *fakeNATSServer) clearActive(conn net.Conn) {
238		s.mu.Lock()
239		defer s.mu.Unlock()
240	
241		if s.active == conn {
242			s.active = nil
243		}
244	}
245	
246	func waitForCondition(timeout time.Duration, fn func() bool) error {
247		deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
248		for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
249			if fn() {
250				return nil
251			}
252			time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
253		}
254	
255		return fmt.Errorf("condition not met within %s", timeout)
256	}

26	func TestWorkerDiscoveryOverNATSDuplicateSessionAndTimeouts(t *testing.T) {
27		t.Parallel()
28	
29		h := newTestAdmin(t)
30		clock := &wireClock{t: time.Unix(1_700_000_000, 0)}
31		reg := NewWorkerRegistry(h.workerCreds, DefaultWorkerTimings(), clock.Now)
32		h.h.Workers = reg
33	
34		pub, priv, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(nil)
35		if err != nil {
36			t.Fatalf("GenerateKey: %v", err)
37		}
38	
39		cred := WorkerCredential{
40			ID:                     "wcred_wire",
41			Status:                 WorkerCredentialStatusActive,
42			ExecutorType:           workerRegTestExecutor,
43			TenantScope:            []string{workerNATSTestTenant},
44			AllowedPools:           []AllowedPool{{TenantID: workerNATSTestTenant, PoolID: routingTestPool1}},
45			PublicKeyEd25519Base64: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(pub),
46		}
47	
48		err = h.workerCreds.Create(context.Background(), cred)
49		if err != nil {
50			t.Fatalf("create worker cred: %v", err)
51		}
52	
53		srv := testutil.NewFakeNATSServer(t, 2_000_000)
54		controlConn := mustConnectNATS(t, srv.URL())
55		t.Cleanup(controlConn.Close)
56	
57		err = SetupWorkerDiscoverySubscriptions(controlConn, reg)
58		if err != nil {
59			t.Fatalf("setup worker discovery: %v", err)
60		}
61	
62		workerConn := mustConnectNATS(t, srv.URL())
63		t.Cleanup(workerConn.Close)
64	
65		id := egress.Identity{
66			WorkerID:     workerRegTestWorker1,
67			CredentialID: cred.ID,
68			ExecutorType: workerRegTestExecutor,
69			PrivateKey:   priv,
70		}
71		caps := egress.Capabilities{
72			AllowedPools:          []strawpb.RegisterRequest_PoolRef{{TenantId: workerNATSTestTenant, PoolId: routingTestPool1}},
73			SoftwareVersion:       workerTestVersion,
74			MaxConcurrency:        4,
75			SupportedIngressModes: []string{"rest"},
76		}
77	
78		sess1, err := egress.Register(context.Background(), workerConn, id, caps)
79		if err != nil {
80			t.Fatalf("first register: %v", err)
81		}
82	
83		err = egress.Heartbeat(context.Background(), workerConn, id, sess1, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, 4, 4, false)
84		if err != nil {
85			t.Fatalf("first heartbeat: %v", err)
86		}
87	
88		platformToken := h.seedPlatformKey(t, "key_admin", RoleSystemAdmin)
89		workers := mustListWorkers(t, h.h, platformToken)
90		if len(workers) != 1 {
91			t.Fatalf("workers = %+v, want 1 row", workers)
92		}
93		if workers[0].SessionID != sess1 || workers[0].RuntimeState != string(RuntimeReady) {
94			t.Fatalf("first worker view = %+v, want ready session %q", workers[0], sess1)
95		}
96	
97		sess2, err := egress.Register(context.Background(), workerConn, id, caps)
98		if err != nil {
99			t.Fatalf("second register: %v", err)
100		}
101		if sess2 == sess1 {
102			t.Fatalf("duplicate registration reused session %q", sess1)
103		}
104	
105		err = egress.Heartbeat(context.Background(), workerConn, id, sess2, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, 4, 4, false)
106		if err != nil {
107			t.Fatalf("second heartbeat: %v", err)
108		}
109	
110		workers = mustListWorkers(t, h.h, platformToken)
111		if workers[0].SessionID != sess2 || workers[0].RuntimeState != string(RuntimeReady) {
112			t.Fatalf("second worker view = %+v, want ready session %q", workers[0], sess2)
113		}
114	
115		err = egress.Heartbeat(context.Background(), workerConn, id, sess1, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, 4, 4, false)
116		if err != nil {
117			t.Fatalf("stale heartbeat request: %v", err)
118		}
119		workers = mustListWorkers(t, h.h, platformToken)
120		if workers[0].SessionID != sess2 || workers[0].RuntimeState != string(RuntimeReady) {
121			t.Fatalf("stale heartbeat changed worker view = %+v", workers[0])
122		}
123	
124		clock.Advance(16 * time.Second)
125		workers = mustListWorkers(t, h.h, platformToken)
126		if workers[0].RuntimeState != string(RuntimeUnavailable) {
127			t.Fatalf("after 16s worker state = %s, want unavailable", workers[0].RuntimeState)
128		}
129	
130		clock.Advance(15 * time.Second)
131		workers = mustListWorkers(t, h.h, platformToken)
132		if workers[0].RuntimeState != string(RuntimeDead) {
133			t.Fatalf("after 31s worker state = %s, want dead", workers[0].RuntimeState)
134		}
135	}

137	func TestWorkerRunLoopAppearsInAdminWorkersAndDrainsOnCancel(t *testing.T) {
138		t.Parallel()
139	
140		h := newTestAdmin(t)
141		clock := &wireClock{t: time.Unix(1_700_000_100, 0)}
142		reg := NewWorkerRegistry(h.workerCreds, DefaultWorkerTimings(), clock.Now)
143		h.h.Workers = reg
144	
145		pub, priv, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(nil)
146		if err != nil {
147			t.Fatalf("GenerateKey: %v", err)
148		}
149	
150		cred := WorkerCredential{
151			ID:                     "wcred_run",
152			Status:                 WorkerCredentialStatusActive,
153			ExecutorType:           workerRegTestExecutor,
154			TenantScope:            []string{workerNATSTestTenant},
155			AllowedPools:           []AllowedPool{{TenantID: workerNATSTestTenant, PoolID: routingTestPool1}},
156			PublicKeyEd25519Base64: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(pub),
157		}
158	
159		err = h.workerCreds.Create(context.Background(), cred)
160		if err != nil {
161			t.Fatalf("create worker cred: %v", err)
162		}
163	
164		srv := testutil.NewFakeNATSServer(t, 2_000_000)
165		controlConn := mustConnectNATS(t, srv.URL())
166		t.Cleanup(controlConn.Close)
167	
168		err = SetupWorkerDiscoverySubscriptions(controlConn, reg)
169		if err != nil {
170			t.Fatalf("setup worker discovery: %v", err)
171		}
172	
173		workerConn := mustConnectNATS(t, srv.URL())
174		t.Cleanup(workerConn.Close)
175	
176		id := egress.Identity{
177			WorkerID:     workerRegTestWorker2,
178			CredentialID: cred.ID,
179			ExecutorType: workerRegTestExecutor,
180			PrivateKey:   priv,
181		}
182		caps := egress.Capabilities{
183			AllowedPools:          []strawpb.RegisterRequest_PoolRef{{TenantId: workerNATSTestTenant, PoolId: routingTestPool1}},
184			SoftwareVersion:       workerTestVersion,
185			MaxConcurrency:        2,
186			SupportedIngressModes: []string{"rest"},
187		}
188	
189		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
190		var runErr error
191		var runMu sync.Mutex
192		done := make(chan struct{})
193		go func() {
194			defer close(done)
195			runErr = egress.Run(ctx, workerConn, id, caps, 25*time.Millisecond)
196			runMu.Lock()
197			defer runMu.Unlock()
198		}()
199	
200		platformToken := h.seedPlatformKey(t, "key_admin", RoleSystemAdmin)
201		waitForWorkerState(t, h.h, platformToken, workerRegTestWorker2, string(RuntimeReady))
202	
203		cancel()
204		<-done
205		if runErr != nil {
206			t.Fatalf("worker run loop error: %v", runErr)
207		}
208	
209		waitForWorkerState(t, h.h, platformToken, workerRegTestWorker2, string(RuntimeDraining))
210	}
211	
212	func mustConnectNATS(t *testing.T, url string) *natsx.Connection {
213		t.Helper()
214	
215		conn, err := natsx.Connect(natsx.ConnectOptions{
216			Servers:         []string{url},
217			ReconnectWait:   10 * time.Millisecond,
218			PingInterval:    100 * time.Millisecond,
219			MaxPingFailures: 1,
220		})
221		if err != nil {
222			t.Fatalf("connect nats: %v", err)
223		}
224	
225		return conn
226	}
227	
228	func mustListWorkers(t *testing.T, h *AdminHandlers, token string) []workerAdminView {
229		t.Helper()
230	
231		req := httptest.NewRequestWithContext(context.Background(), http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/admin/workers", nil)
232		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
233		w := httptest.NewRecorder()
234		h.ListWorkers(w, req)
235		if w.Code != http.StatusOK {
236			t.Fatalf("list workers status = %d body=%s", w.Code, w.Body.String())
237		}
238	
239		var views []workerAdminView
240	
241		err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &views)
242		if err != nil {
243			t.Fatalf("unmarshal workers: %v", err)
244		}
245	
246		return views
247	}
248	
249	func waitForWorkerState(t *testing.T, h *AdminHandlers, token, workerID, want string) {
250		t.Helper()
251	
252		deadline := time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second)
253		for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
254			for _, row := range mustListWorkers(t, h, token) {
255				if row.WorkerID == workerID && row.RuntimeState == want {
256					return
257				}
258			}
259			time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
260		}
261	
262		t.Fatalf("worker %s did not reach state %s", workerID, want)
263	}
264	
265	type wireClock struct {
266		mu sync.Mutex
267		t  time.Time
268	}
269	
270	func (c *wireClock) Now() time.Time {
271		c.mu.Lock()
272		defer c.mu.Unlock()
273	
274		return c.t
275	}
276	
277	func (c *wireClock) Advance(d time.Duration) {
278		c.mu.Lock()
279		defer c.mu.Unlock()
280	
281		c.t = c.t.Add(d)
282	}

15	const (

17		fakeNATSFieldsUNSUB = 2
18		fakeNATSFieldsPUB   = 3
19		fakeNATSMaxPayload  = 2
20	)
21	
22	// FakeNATSServer is a tiny Core NATS broker for tests that need real request/
23	// reply and subscription routing without an external process.
24	type FakeNATSServer struct {
25		ln         net.Listener
26		maxPayload int
27	
28		mu        sync.Mutex
29		clients   map[*fakeNATSClient]struct{}
30		bySubject map[string]map[*fakeNATSSubscription]struct{}
31	}
32	
33	type fakeNATSClient struct {
34		server *FakeNATSServer
35		conn   net.Conn
36	
37		wmu  sync.Mutex
38		mu   sync.Mutex
39		subs map[string]*fakeNATSSubscription
40	}
41	
42	type fakeNATSSubscription struct {
43		client  *fakeNATSClient
44		subject string
45		queue   string
46		sid     string
47	}
48	
49	// ErrInvalidSUB, ErrInvalidUNSUB, ErrInvalidPUB, ErrInvalidPUBSize are sentinel errors for malformed NATS protocol lines.
50	var (
51		ErrInvalidSUB   = errors.New("invalid SUB line")
52		ErrInvalidUNSUB = errors.New("invalid UNSUB line")
53		ErrInvalidPUB   = errors.New("invalid PUB line")
54		ErrInvalidPUBSz = errors.New("invalid PUB size")
55	)
56	
57	// NewFakeNATSServer starts a broker on 127.0.0.1 and registers cleanup with t.
58	func NewFakeNATSServer(t testing.TB, maxPayload int) *FakeNATSServer {
59		t.Helper()
60	
61		lc := net.ListenConfig{}
62	
63		ln, err := lc.Listen(context.Background(), "tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
64		if err != nil {
65			t.Fatalf("listen fake NATS: %v", err)
66		}
67	
68		srv := &FakeNATSServer{
69			ln:         ln,
70			maxPayload: maxPayload,
71			clients:    make(map[*fakeNATSClient]struct{}),
72			bySubject:  make(map[string]map[*fakeNATSSubscription]struct{}),
73		}
74		go srv.acceptLoop()
75	
76		t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
77	
78		return srv
79	}
80	
81	// URL returns the broker URL for NATS clients.
82	func (s *FakeNATSServer) URL() string {
83		return "nats://" + s.ln.Addr().String()
84	}
85	
86	// Close shuts down the listener and all active clients.
87	func (s *FakeNATSServer) Close() {
88		_ = s.ln.Close()
89	
90		s.mu.Lock()
91	
92		clients := make([]*fakeNATSClient, 0, len(s.clients))
93		for c := range s.clients {
94			clients = append(clients, c)
95		}
96		s.mu.Unlock()
97	
98		for _, c := range clients {
99			_ = c.conn.Close()
100		}
101	}
102	
103	func (s *FakeNATSServer) acceptLoop() {
104		for {
105			conn, err := s.ln.Accept()
106			if err != nil {
107				return
108			}
109	
110			client := &fakeNATSClient{
111				server: s,
112				conn:   conn,
113				subs:   make(map[string]*fakeNATSSubscription),
114			}
115			s.mu.Lock()
116			s.clients[client] = struct{}{}
117			s.mu.Unlock()
118	
119			go client.serve()
120		}
121	}
122	
123	func (c *fakeNATSClient) serve() {
124		defer c.close()
125	
126		err := c.writeInfo()
127		if err != nil {
128			return
129		}
130	
131		reader := bufio.NewReader(c.conn)
132		for {
133			line, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
134			if err != nil {
135				return
136			}
137	
138			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
139			if line == "" {
140				continue
141			}
142	
143			err = c.handleLine(line, reader)
144			if err != nil {
145				return
146			}
147		}
148	}
149	
150	func (c *fakeNATSClient) handleLine(line string, reader *bufio.Reader) error {
151		switch {
152		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "PING"):
153			return c.writeString("PONG\r\n")
154		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "PONG"):
155			return nil
156		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "CONNECT"):
157			return nil
158		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "SUB "):
159			return c.handleSub(line)
160		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "UNSUB "):
161			return c.handleUnsub(line)
162		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "PUB "):
163			return c.handlePub(line, reader)
164		default:
165			return nil
166		}
167	}
168	
169	func (c *fakeNATSClient) writeInfo() error {
170		info := fmt.Sprintf(
171			`INFO {"server_id":"straw-test","version":"1.0.0","proto":1,"max_payload":%d}`+"\r\n",
172			c.server.maxPayload,
173		)
174	
175		return c.writeString(info)
176	}
177	
178	func (c *fakeNATSClient) handleSub(line string) error {
179		fields := strings.Fields(line)
180		if len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsSUB && len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsSUB+1 {
181			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrInvalidSUB, line)
182		}
183	
184		subject := fields[1]
185		queue := ""
186	
187		sid := fields[len(fields)-1]
188		if len(fields) == fakeNATSFieldsSUB+1 {
189			queue = fields[2]
190		}
191	
192		sub := &fakeNATSSubscription{client: c, subject: subject, queue: queue, sid: sid}
193		c.mu.Lock()
194		c.subs[sid] = sub
195		c.mu.Unlock()
196	
197		c.server.mu.Lock()
198		defer c.server.mu.Unlock()
199	
200		if c.server.bySubject[subject] == nil {
201			c.server.bySubject[subject] = make(map[*fakeNATSSubscription]struct{})
202		}
203	
204		c.server.bySubject[subject][sub] = struct{}{}
205	
206		return nil
207	}
208	
209	func (c *fakeNATSClient) handleUnsub(line string) error {
210		fields := strings.Fields(line)
211		if len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsUNSUB && len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsUNSUB+1 {
212			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrInvalidUNSUB, line)
213		}
214	
215		sid := fields[1]
216	
217		c.mu.Lock()
218		sub := c.subs[sid]
219		delete(c.subs, sid)
220		c.mu.Unlock()
221	
222		if sub == nil {
223			return nil
224		}
225	
226		c.server.mu.Lock()
227		defer c.server.mu.Unlock()
228	
229		if subs := c.server.bySubject[sub.subject]; subs != nil {
230			delete(subs, sub)
231	
232			if len(subs) == 0 {
233				delete(c.server.bySubject, sub.subject)
234			}
235		}
236	
237		return nil
238	}
239	
240	func (c *fakeNATSClient) handlePub(line string, reader *bufio.Reader) error {
241		fields := strings.Fields(line)
242		if len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsPUB && len(fields) != fakeNATSFieldsPUB+1 {
243			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrInvalidPUB, line)
244		}
245	
246		subject := fields[1]
247		replyTo := ""
248	
249		sizeField := fields[len(fields)-1]
250		if len(fields) == fakeNATSFieldsPUB+1 {
251			replyTo = fields[2]
252		}
253	
254		var size int
255	
256		_, err := fmt.Sscanf(sizeField, "%d", &size)
257		if err != nil || size < 0 {
258			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrInvalidPUBSz, line)
259		}
260	
261		payload := make([]byte, size)
262	
263		_, err = io.ReadFull(reader, payload)
264		if err != nil {
265			return fmt.Errorf("read payload: %w", err)
266		}
267	
268		trailer := make([]byte, fakeNATSMaxPayload)
269	
270		_, err = io.ReadFull(reader, trailer)
271		if err != nil {
272			return fmt.Errorf("read trailer: %w", err)
273		}
274	
275		c.server.publish(subject, replyTo, payload)
276	
277		return nil
278	}
279	
280	func (s *FakeNATSServer) publish(subject, replyTo string, payload []byte) {
281		s.mu.Lock()
282		direct := make([]*fakeNATSSubscription, 0)
283		queueTargets := make(map[string]*fakeNATSSubscription)
284	
285		for pattern, subs := range s.bySubject {
286			if !subjectMatches(pattern, subject) {
287				continue
288			}
289	
290			for sub := range subs {
291				if sub.queue == "" {
292					direct = append(direct, sub)
293	
294					continue
295				}
296	
297				key := pattern + "\x00" + sub.queue
298				if _, ok := queueTargets[key]; !ok {
299					queueTargets[key] = sub
300				}
301			}
302		}
303	
304		targets := direct
305		for _, sub := range queueTargets {
306			targets = append(targets, sub)
307		}
308		s.mu.Unlock()
309	
310		for _, sub := range targets {
311			err := sub.client.writeMsg(subject, sub.sid, replyTo, payload)
312			if err != nil {
313				_ = sub.client.conn.Close()
314			}
315		}
316	}
317	
318	func subjectMatches(pattern, subject string) bool {
319		if pattern == subject {
320			return true
321		}
322	
323		pp := strings.Split(pattern, ".")
324		sp := strings.Split(subject, ".")
325	
326		for i := range pp {
327			if pp[i] == ">" {
328				return i == len(pp)-1
329			}
330	
331			if i >= len(sp) {
332				return false
333			}
334	
335			if pp[i] != "*" && pp[i] != sp[i] {
336				return false
337			}
338		}
339	
340		return len(pp) == len(sp)
341	}
342	
343	func (c *fakeNATSClient) writeMsg(subject, sid, replyTo string, payload []byte) error {
344		c.wmu.Lock()
345		defer c.wmu.Unlock()
346	
347		var err error
348	
349		if replyTo == "" {
350			_, err = fmt.Fprintf(c.conn, "MSG %s %s %d\r\n", subject, sid, len(payload))
351			if err != nil {
352				return fmt.Errorf("write msg: %w", err)
353			}
354		} else {
355			_, err = fmt.Fprintf(c.conn, "MSG %s %s %s %d\r\n", subject, sid, replyTo, len(payload))
356			if err != nil {
357				return fmt.Errorf("write msg: %w", err)
358			}
359		}
360	
361		_, err = c.conn.Write(payload)
362		if err != nil {
363			return fmt.Errorf("write payload: %w", err)
364		}
365	
366		_, err = c.conn.Write([]byte("\r\n"))
367		if err != nil {
368			return fmt.Errorf("write trailing newline: %w", err)
369		}
370	
371		return nil
372	}
373	
374	func (c *fakeNATSClient) writeString(s string) error {
375		c.wmu.Lock()
376		defer c.wmu.Unlock()
377	
378		_, err := io.WriteString(c.conn, s)
379		if err != nil {
380			return fmt.Errorf("write string: %w", err)
381		}
382	
383		return nil
384	}
385	
386	func (c *fakeNATSClient) close() {
387		c.server.mu.Lock()
388		delete(c.server.clients, c)
389	
390		subs := make([]*fakeNATSSubscription, 0, len(c.subs))
391		for _, sub := range c.subs {
392			subs = append(subs, sub)
393		}
394		c.server.mu.Unlock()
395	
396		for _, sub := range subs {
397			c.server.mu.Lock()
398			if subjectSubs := c.server.bySubject[sub.subject]; subjectSubs != nil {
399				delete(subjectSubs, sub)
400	
401				if len(subjectSubs) == 0 {
402					delete(c.server.bySubject, sub.subject)
403				}
404			}
405			c.server.mu.Unlock()
406		}
407	
408		_ = c.conn.Close()
409	}

19	func TestExecutorEmitsSuccessfulHTTPFramesAndAppliesInjection(t *testing.T) {
20		t.Parallel()
21	
22		server := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
23			if r.Header.Get("X-Set") != "two" {
24				t.Fatalf("X-Set = %q, want two", r.Header.Get("X-Set"))
25			}
26			if got := r.Header.Values("X-Append"); strings.Join(got, ",") != "one,two" {
27				t.Fatalf("X-Append = %q, want one,two", got)
28			}
29			if r.Header.Get("X-Remove") != "" {
30				t.Fatalf("X-Remove = %q, want empty", r.Header.Get("X-Remove"))
31			}
32			if r.Header.Get("Cookie") != "session=abc" {
33				t.Fatalf("Cookie = %q, want session=abc", r.Header.Get("Cookie"))
34			}
35			body, err := io.ReadAll(r.Body)
36			if err != nil {
37				t.Fatalf("read body: %v", err)
38			}
39			if string(body) != "payload" {
40				t.Fatalf("body = %q, want payload", body)
41			}
42	
43			w.Header().Set("X-Origin", "ok")
44			w.WriteHeader(http.StatusTeapot)
45			_, _ = w.Write([]byte("ok"))
46		}))
47		t.Cleanup(server.Close)
48	
49		target := rewriteHost(t, server.URL, "unit.test")
50		exec := NewExecutor(ExecutorOptions{Resolver: staticResolver{"unit.test": loopbackIP(t, server.URL)}})
51		frames := exec.Execute(context.Background(), requestStart(target, directPolicy(true)), []byte("payload"), 3)
52	
53		if len(frames) != 4 {
54			t.Fatalf("len(frames) = %d, want 4", len(frames))
55		}
56		if got := frames[0].GetOutboundStart(); got == nil || got.GetTargetHost() != "unit.test" || got.GetAttempt() != 3 {
57			t.Fatalf("outbound start = %#v", got)
58		}
59		if got := frames[1].GetResponseStart(); got == nil || got.GetStatus() != http.StatusTeapot {
60			t.Fatalf("response start = %#v", got)
61		}
62		if got := string(frames[2].GetData().GetData()); got != "ok" {
63			t.Fatalf("data = %q, want ok", got)
64		}
65		if got := frames[3].GetEnd(); got == nil || !got.GetSuccess() {
66			t.Fatalf("end = %#v", got)
67		}
68	}
69	
70	func TestExecutorRejectsResolvedDeniedIPAndRedactsDetails(t *testing.T) {
71		t.Parallel()
72	
73		dialed := false
74		exec := NewExecutor(ExecutorOptions{
75			Resolver: staticResolver{"metadata.test": netip.MustParseAddr("169.254.169.254")},
76			DialContext: func(context.Context, string, string) (net.Conn, error) {
77				dialed = true
78	
79				return nil, errors.New("unexpected dial")
80			},
81		})
82		start := requestStart("http://metadata.test/secret?token=abc", directPolicy(false))
83		start.Headers = []*strawpb.Header{{Name: "Authorization", Value: []byte("secret")}}
84	
85		frames := exec.Execute(context.Background(), start, nil, 1)
86	
87		if dialed {
88			t.Fatal("executor dialed a denied destination")
89		}
90		errFrame := terminalError(t, frames)
91		if errFrame.GetCode() != strawpb.ErrorCode_ERROR_CODE_DESTINATION_DENIED {
92			t.Fatalf("code = %v, want destination_denied", errFrame.GetCode())
93		}
94		if got := errFrame.GetDetails(); len(got) != 1 || got["fact"] != "dns_denied_ip" {
95			t.Fatalf("details = %#v, want only fact=dns_denied_ip", got)
96		}
97	}

10	func TestStreamValidatorRules(t *testing.T) {
11		t.Parallel()
12	
13		now := time.Unix(100, 0)
14		v := NewStreamValidator(3, 5, time.Second, func() time.Time { return now })
15	
16		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 2, Attempt: 3, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_End{End: &strawpb.EndFrame{Success: true}}}); got != FrameSequenceGap {
17			t.Fatalf("gap outcome = %v, want FrameSequenceGap", got)
18		}
19		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 0, Attempt: 3, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_End{End: &strawpb.EndFrame{Success: true}}}); got != FrameInvalid {
20			t.Fatalf("zero seq outcome = %v, want FrameInvalid", got)
21		}
22		frame := &strawpb.StreamFrame{
23			StreamSeq: 1,
24			Attempt:   3,
25			Payload:   &strawpb.StreamFrame_Data{Data: &strawpb.DataFrame{Offset: 0, Data: []byte("abc")}},
26		}
27		if got := v.Accept(frame); got != FrameAccepted {
28			t.Fatalf("data accept outcome = %v, want accepted", got)
29		}
30		if got := v.Accept(frame); got != FrameDuplicate {
31			t.Fatalf("duplicate outcome = %v, want duplicate", got)
32		}
33		if v.RemainingCredit() != 2 || v.ExpectedSeq() != 2 {
34			t.Fatalf("state after data = credit %d seq %d, want 2/2", v.RemainingCredit(), v.ExpectedSeq())
35		}
36		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 2, Attempt: 3, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_End{End: &strawpb.EndFrame{Success: true}}}); got != FrameAccepted || !v.Terminated() {
37			t.Fatalf("terminal outcome = %v terminated=%v, want accepted/true", got, v.Terminated())
38		}
39		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 3, Attempt: 3, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_Data{Data: &strawpb.DataFrame{Offset: 3, Data: []byte("z")}}}); got != FrameAfterTerminal {
40			t.Fatalf("late frame outcome = %v, want FrameAfterTerminal", got)
41		}
42	}
43	
44	func TestStreamValidatorCreditOffsetAndIdle(t *testing.T) {
45		t.Parallel()
46	
47		now := time.Unix(200, 0)
48		current := now
49		v := NewStreamValidator(1, 1, time.Second, func() time.Time { return current })
50	
51		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 1, Attempt: 1, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_Data{Data: &strawpb.DataFrame{Offset: 1, Data: []byte("x")}}}); got != FrameOffsetMismatch {
52			t.Fatalf("offset mismatch outcome = %v, want FrameOffsetMismatch", got)
53		}
54		if got := v.Accept(&strawpb.StreamFrame{StreamSeq: 1, Attempt: 1, Payload: &strawpb.StreamFrame_Data{Data: &strawpb.DataFrame{Offset: 0, Data: []byte("xy")}}}); got != FrameCreditExhausted {
55			t.Fatalf("credit exhaustion outcome = %v, want FrameCreditExhausted", got)
56		}
57		current = current.Add(time.Second)
58		if !v.IdleExpired() {
59			t.Fatal("IdleExpired() = false, want true after timeout")
60		}
61	}

  "content": "package egress\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/nats-io/nats.go\"\n\n\tstrawpb \"github.com/beremaran/straw/v2/api/proto/straw/v1\"\n\t\"github.com/beremaran/straw/v2/internal/natsx\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\t// frameIdleTimeout is the P0 default from\n\t// docs/planning/12-nats-protocol.md (\"control.transport.frame_idle_timeout_ms\").\n\tframeIdleTimeout = 15 * time.Second\n\tidleCheckInterval = 500 * time.Millisecond\n\t// workerDrainTimeout mirrors the Control drain timeout default in\n\t// docs/planning/29-operational-behavior.md; egress has no separate\n\t// configured default yet.\n\tworkerDrainTimeout       = 60 * time.Second\n\tstreamFrameChannelBuffer = 32\n)\n\nvar errExecutorRequired = errors.New(\"executor is required\")\n\n// p0SupportedModes is the set of RequestModes the official Go worker accepts\n// in P0 (docs/planning/16: no BodyRef, provider adapters, or raw tunnels yet).\nvar p0SupportedModes = []strawpb.RequestMode{strawpb.RequestMode_REQUEST_MODE_DECODED_HTTP}\n\n// Worker runs the live NATS assignment execution loop for one registered\n// worker session (docs/planning/12 \"Assignment Flow\", docs/planning/09 steps\n// 13-21).\ntype Worker struct {\n\tconn      *natsx.Connection\n\tid        Identity\n\texecutor  *Executor\n\tsessionID string\n\n\tmaxConcurrency uint32\n\n\tmu       sync.Mutex\n\tactive   uint32\n\tdraining bool\n\tcancels  map[string]context.CancelFunc\n\n\twg sync.WaitGroup\n}\n\n// NewWorker builds a Worker bound to a live registered session.\nfunc NewWorker(conn *natsx.Connection, id Identity, executor *Executor, sessionID string, maxConcurrency uint32) (*Worker, error) {\n\tif conn == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, errConnRequired\n\t}\n\n\tif executor == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, errExecutorRequired\n\t}\n\n\terr := natsx.ValidateSubjectToken(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"session_id: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &Worker{\n\t\tconn:           conn,\n\t\tid:             id,\n\t\texecutor:       executor,\n\t\tsessionID:      sessionID,\n\t\tmaxConcurrency: maxConcurrency,\n\t\tcancels:        make(map[string]context.CancelFunc),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// ActiveRequests reports the number of assignments currently executing.\nfunc (w *Worker) ActiveRequests() uint32 {\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer w.mu.Unlock()\n\n\treturn w.active\n}\n\n// Serve subscribes to this worker's exact-session assignment subject (never a\n// queue group, per docs/planning/12) and processes assignments until ctx is\n// canceled. On cancellation it stops accepting new assignments and drains\n// in-flight requests until they finish or workerDrainTimeout elapses\n// (docs/planning/29 \"Worker Graceful Shutdown\", steps 2-3).\nfunc (w *Worker) Serve(ctx context.Context) error {\n\tsubject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"assignment subject: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tsub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(subject, w.handleAssign)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"subscribe assignment: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\terr = w.conn.Flush()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = sub.Unsubscribe()\n\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"flush assignment subscription: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t<-ctx.Done()\n\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tw.draining = true\n\tw.mu.Unlock()\n\n\t_ = sub.Unsubscribe()\n\n\tdrained := make(chan struct{})\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tw.wg.Wait()\n\t\tclose(drained)\n\t}()\n\n\tselect {\n\tcase <-drained:\n\tcase <-time.After(workerDrainTimeout):\n\t\tw.abandonInFlight()\n\t\t<-drained\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) abandonInFlight() {\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tcancels := make([]context.CancelFunc, 0, len(w.cancels))\n\tfor _, cancel := range w.cancels {\n\t\tcancels = append(cancels, cancel)\n\t}\n\tw.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tfor _, cancel := range cancels {\n\t\tcancel()\n\t}\n}\n\n// handleAssign implements the executor side of the assignment flow\n// (docs/planning/12 \"Assignment Flow\", steps 3-5): evaluate and reserve\n// capacity, subscribe and flush the request-scoped c2e subject before\n// accepting, then reply with AssignAck.\nfunc (w *Worker) handleAssign(msg *nats.Msg) {\n\tenv, decodeErr := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(msg.Data)\n\tif decodeErr != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, nil, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\treq := env.GetAssignRequest()\n\n\tcode := EvaluateAssignment(req, w.snapshotCapacity())\n\tif code != strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: code})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\trequestID := env.GetRequestId()\n\n\tc2eSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionControlToExecutor)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\te2cSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionExecutorToControl)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tframes := make(chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, streamFrameChannelBuffer)\n\n\tc2eSub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(c2eSubject, func(m *nats.Msg) {\n\t\tframes <- decodeStreamFrame(m.Data)\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\terr = w.conn.Flush()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe()\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\treqCtx, cancel := requestContext(env.GetDeadlineUnixMs())\n\tw.reserve(requestID, cancel)\n\n\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED})\n\n\tw.wg.Add(1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer w.wg.Done()\n\t\tdefer func() { _ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe() }()\n\t\tdefer w.release(requestID)\n\n\t\tw.runRequest(reqCtx, cancel, req, env, frames, e2cSubject)\n\t}()\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) snapshotCapacity() Capacity {\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer w.mu.Unlock()\n\n\treturn Capacity{\n\t\tDraining:       w.draining,\n\t\tActiveRequests: w.active,\n\t\tMaxConcurrency: w.maxConcurrency,\n\t\tSupportedModes: p0SupportedModes,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) reserve(requestID string, cancel context.CancelFunc) {\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer w.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tw.active++\n\tw.cancels[requestID] = cancel\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) release(requestID string) {\n\tw.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer w.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tw.active--\n\tdelete(w.cancels, requestID)\n}\n\nfunc requestContext(deadlineUnixMs int64) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {\n\tif deadlineUnixMs <= 0 {\n\t\treturn context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\t}\n\n\treturn context.WithDeadline(context.Background(), time.UnixMilli(deadlineUnixMs))\n}\n\n// runRequest waits for RequestStart and its inline body over the c2e stream,\n// executes the outbound request, and publishes the resulting frames to e2c\n// (docs/planning/09 steps 16-21). If RequestStart never arrives (deadline,\n// protocol error, or a pre-start Cancel), nothing is published: Control\n// synthesizes the terminal outcome per docs/planning/09 \"Terminal Rule\".\nfunc (w *Worker) runRequest(reqCtx context.Context, cancel context.CancelFunc, req *strawpb.AssignRequest, env *strawpb.Envelope, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, e2cSubject string) {\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tvalidator := natsx.NewStreamValidator(req.GetAttempt(), req.GetInitialUploadCreditBytes(), frameIdleTimeout, nil)\n\n\tstart, body, ok := readRequestBody(reqCtx, validator, frames, req.GetExpectedUploadBytes())\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tresultCh := make(chan []*strawpb.StreamFrame, 1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tresultCh <- w.executor.Execute(reqCtx, start, body, req.GetAttempt())\n\t}()\n\n\tresult, canceled, reason := waitForResult(resultCh, frames, validator, cancel)\n\tif canceled {\n\t\tresult = applyCancellation(result, reason)\n\t}\n\n\tw.publish(e2cSubject, env, result)\n}\n\n// readRequestBody accepts c2e frames until RequestStart and its full inline\n// body (sized by AssignRequest.ExpectedUploadBytes) have arrived. P0 does not\n// support BodyRef (docs/planning/16), so the body is always inline DataFrames.\nfunc readRequestBody(ctx context.Context, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, expectedUploadBytes int64) (*strawpb.RequestStart, []byte, bool) {\n\tvar (\n\t\tstart    *strawpb.RequestStart\n\t\tbody     []byte\n\t\treceived uint64\n\t)\n\n\tvar expected uint64\n\tif expectedUploadBytes > 0 {\n\t\texpected = uint64(expectedUploadBytes)\n\t}\n\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(idleCheckInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor start == nil || received < expected {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\tcase frame, ok := <-frames:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\toutcome := validator.Accept(frame)\n\t\t\tif outcome == natsx.FrameDuplicate {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif outcome != natsx.FrameAccepted {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tswitch p := frame.GetPayload().(type) {\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_RequestStart:\n\t\t\t\tstart = p.RequestStart\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Data:\n\t\t\t\tbody = append(body, p.Data.GetData()...)\n\t\t\t\treceived += uint64(len(p.Data.GetData()))\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Credit:\n\t\t\t\t// Download credit grant for e2c response bytes. P0 executes\n\t\t\t\t// the whole response in one pass (Executor.Execute) rather\n\t\t\t\t// than chunking by credit; response buffering per credit is\n\t\t\t\t// Control's responsibility (docs/tasks/p0/24).\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tif validator.IdleExpired() {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn start, body, true\n}\n\n// waitForResult waits for the outbound execution to finish while still\n// accepting c2e frames, most importantly a CancelFrame, which cancels\n// execCtx so Executor.Execute aborts promptly.\nfunc waitForResult(resultCh <-chan []*strawpb.StreamFrame, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, cancel context.CancelFunc) ([]*strawpb.StreamFrame, bool, string) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase result := <-resultCh:\n\t\t\treturn result, false, \"\"\n\t\tcase frame, ok := <-frames:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif validator.Accept(frame) != natsx.FrameAccepted {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tcancelFrame, isCancel := frame.GetPayload().(*strawpb.StreamFrame_Cancel)\n\t\t\tif !isCancel {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tcancel()\n\n\t\t\treturn <-resultCh, true, cancelFrame.Cancel.GetReason()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// applyCancellation replaces the terminal frame of a Cancel-aborted execution\n// with a CancelledFrame (docs/planning/09 \"Terminal Rule\"), unless execution\n// had already completed successfully before the cancel took effect\n// (cancellation is best effort).\nfunc applyCancellation(frames []*strawpb.StreamFrame, reason string) []*strawpb.StreamFrame {\n\tif len(frames) == 0 {\n\t\treturn frames\n\t}\n\n\tlast := frames[len(frames)-1]\n\tif _, ok := last.GetPayload().(*strawpb.StreamFrame_End); ok {\n\t\treturn frames\n\t}\n\n\tframes[len(frames)-1] = &strawpb.StreamFrame{\n\t\tStreamSeq: last.GetStreamSeq(),\n\t\tAttempt:   last.GetAttempt(),\n\t\tPayload:   &strawpb.StreamFrame_Cancelled{Cancelled: &strawpb.CancelledFrame{Reason: reason}},\n\t}\n\n\treturn frames\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) publish(subject string, env *strawpb.Envelope, frames []*strawpb.StreamFrame) {\n\tfor _, frame := range frames {\n\t\tout := &strawpb.Envelope{\n\t\t\tRequestId:      env.GetRequestId(),\n\t\t\tTenantId:       env.GetTenantId(),\n\t\t\tTraceId:        env.GetTraceId(),\n\t\t\tDeadlineUnixMs: env.GetDeadlineUnixMs(),\n\t\t\tProtocolMajor:  ProtocolMajor,\n\t\t\tAttempt:        env.GetAttempt(),\n\t\t\tPayload:        &strawpb.Envelope_StreamFrame{StreamFrame: frame},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\traw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(out)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\terr = w.conn.Publish(subject, raw)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc decodeStreamFrame(raw []byte) *strawpb.StreamFrame {\n\tenv, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(raw)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn env.GetStreamFrame()\n}\n\nfunc (w *Worker) reply(msg *nats.Msg, env *strawpb.Envelope, ack *strawpb.AssignAck) {\n\treply := &strawpb.Envelope{\n\t\tProtocolMajor: ProtocolMajor,\n\t\tPayload:       &strawpb.Envelope_AssignAck{AssignAck: ack},\n\t}\n\n\tif env != nil {\n\t\treply.RequestId = env.GetRequestId()\n\t\treply.TenantId = env.GetTenantId()\n\t\treply.TraceId = env.GetTraceId()\n\t\treply.DeadlineUnixMs = env.GetDeadlineUnixMs()\n\t\treply.Attempt = env.GetAttempt()\n\t}\n\n\traw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(reply)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t_ = msg.Respond(raw)\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "// Run keeps the worker registered and heartbeating until ctx is canceled.\nfunc Run(ctx context.Context, conn *natsx.Connection, id Identity, caps Capabilities, heartbeatInterval time.Duration) error {\n\tif heartbeatInterval <= 0 {\n\t\theartbeatInterval = defaultHeartbeatInterval\n\t}\n\n\tsessionID, err := Register(ctx, conn, id, caps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tavailableCapacity := capacityFromConcurrency(0, caps.MaxConcurrency)\n\n\terr = Heartbeat(ctx, conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, availableCapacity, caps.MaxConcurrency, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(heartbeatInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t_ = Heartbeat(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, availableCapacity, caps.MaxConcurrency, true)\n\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\t_ = Heartbeat(ctx, conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, 0, availableCapacity, caps.MaxConcurrency, false)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// Run keeps the worker registered and heartbeating, and runs the live\n// assignment execution loop, until ctx is canceled. On shutdown it sends a\n// draining heartbeat before telling the assignment loop to stop accepting\n// new work and drain in-flight requests (docs/planning/29 \"Worker Graceful\n// Shutdown\").\nfunc Run(ctx context.Context, conn *natsx.Connection, id Identity, caps Capabilities, executor *Executor, heartbeatInterval time.Duration) error {\n\tif heartbeatInterval <= 0 {\n\t\theartbeatInterval = defaultHeartbeatInterval\n\t}\n\n\tsessionID, err := Register(ctx, conn, id, caps)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tworker, err := NewWorker(conn, id, executor, sessionID, caps.MaxConcurrency)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tserveCtx, stopServing := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer stopServing()\n\n\tserveDone := make(chan error, 1)\n\n\tgo func() { serveDone <- worker.Serve(serveCtx) }()\n\n\tactive := worker.ActiveRequests()\n\n\terr = Heartbeat(ctx, conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, active, capacityFromConcurrency(active, caps.MaxConcurrency), caps.MaxConcurrency, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tstopServing()\n\t\t<-serveDone\n\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(heartbeatInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\tactive = worker.ActiveRequests()\n\t\t\t_ = Heartbeat(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, active, capacityFromConcurrency(active, caps.MaxConcurrency), caps.MaxConcurrency, true)\n\n\t\t\tstopServing()\n\t\t\t<-serveDone\n\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tactive = worker.ActiveRequests()\n\t\t\t_ = Heartbeat(ctx, conn, id, sessionID, strawpb.WorkerHealth_WORKER_HEALTH_READY, active, capacityFromConcurrency(active, caps.MaxConcurrency), caps.MaxConcurrency, false)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}"
}

250	type staticResolver map[string]netip.Addr

252	func (r staticResolver) LookupIPAddr(context.Context, string) ([]net.IPAddr, error) {
253		out := make([]net.IPAddr, 0, len(r))
254		for _, addr := range r {
255			out = append(out, net.IPAddr{IP: net.ParseIP(addr.String())})
256		}
257	
258		return out, nil
259	}

261	func requestStart(rawURL string, policy *strawpb.DestinationPolicy) *strawpb.RequestStart {
262		return &strawpb.RequestStart{
263			Mode:           strawpb.RequestMode_REQUEST_MODE_DECODED_HTTP,
264			Method:         http.MethodPost,
265			Url:            rawURL,
266			DeadlineUnixMs: time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second).UnixMilli(),

268			InjectionOperations: []*strawpb.InjectionOperation{
269				{Op: opSet, HeaderName: "X-Set", Value: []byte("two")},
270				{Op: opSet, HeaderName: "Cookie", Value: []byte("session=abc")},
271				{Op: opAppend, HeaderName: "X-Append", Value: []byte("two")},
272				{Op: opRemove, HeaderName: "X-Remove"},
273			},
274			DestinationPolicy: policy,
275		}
276	}
