242	
243	// loopHarness drives a live Worker over a real (fake) NATS broker, playing
244	// Control's side of the assignment/stream protocol.
245	type loopHarness struct {
246		workerConn  *natsx.Connection
247		controlConn *natsx.Connection
248		worker      *Worker
249		cancel      context.CancelFunc
250	
251		e2cSubs map[string]chan *strawpb.StreamFrame
252	}
253	
254	func newLoopHarness(t *testing.T, executor *Executor, maxConcurrency uint32) *loopHarness {
255		t.Helper()
256	
257		srv := testutil.NewFakeNATSServer(t, 2_000_000)
258		workerConn := loopConnect(t, srv.URL())
259		controlConn := loopConnect(t, srv.URL())
260	
261		worker, err := NewWorker(workerConn, Identity{WorkerID: loopTestWorker}, executor, loopTestSession, maxConcurrency)
262		if err != nil {
263			t.Fatalf("NewWorker() error = %v", err)
264		}
265	
266		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
267		t.Cleanup(cancel)
268	
269		serveDone := make(chan struct{})
270		go func() {
271			defer close(serveDone)
272			_ = worker.Serve(ctx)
273		}()
274		t.Cleanup(func() { <-serveDone })
275	
276		return &loopHarness{
277			workerConn:  workerConn,
278			controlConn: controlConn,
279			worker:      worker,
280			cancel:      cancel,
281			e2cSubs:     make(map[string]chan *strawpb.StreamFrame),
282		}
283	}
284	
285	func (h *loopHarness) assign(t *testing.T, requestID string, req strawpb.AssignRequest) *strawpb.AssignAck {
286		t.Helper()
287	
288		// Subscribe and flush the request-scoped e2c subject before sending the
289		// AssignRequest, mirroring Control's own ordering obligation.
290		h.subscribeE2C(t, requestID)
291	
292		subject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(loopTestWorker, loopTestSession)
293		if err != nil {
294			t.Fatalf("AssignmentSubject() error = %v", err)
295		}
296	
297		reqCopy := req
298		env := &strawpb.Envelope{
299			RequestId:      requestID,
300			TenantId:       loopTestTenant,
301			DeadlineUnixMs: req.GetDeadlineUnixMs(),
302			ProtocolMajor:  ProtocolMajor,
303			Attempt:        req.GetAttempt(),
304			Payload:        &strawpb.Envelope_AssignRequest{AssignRequest: &reqCopy},
305		}
306	
307		raw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(env)
308		if err != nil {
309			t.Fatalf("MarshalEnvelope() error = %v", err)
310		}
311	
312		var reply []byte
313	
314		deadline := time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second)
315		for {
316			msg, reqErr := h.controlConn.Request(subject, raw, 200*time.Millisecond)
317			if reqErr == nil {
318				reply = msg.Data
319	
320				break
321			}
322	
323			if time.Now().After(deadline) {
324				t.Fatalf("assign request %s: %v", requestID, reqErr)
325			}
326		}
327	
328		replyEnv, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(reply)
329		if err != nil {
330			t.Fatalf("UnmarshalEnvelope(reply) error = %v", err)
331		}
332	
333		return replyEnv.GetAssignAck()

287	func (h *loopHarness) assign(t *testing.T, requestID string, req strawpb.AssignRequest) *strawpb.AssignAck {
288		t.Helper()
289	
290		// Subscribe and flush the request-scoped e2c subject before sending the
291		// AssignRequest, mirroring Control's own ordering obligation.
292		h.subscribeE2C(t, requestID)
293	
294		subject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(loopTestWorker, loopTestSession)
295		if err != nil {
296			t.Fatalf("AssignmentSubject() error = %v", err)
297		}
298	
299		reqCopy := req
300		env := &strawpb.Envelope{
301			RequestId:      requestID,
302			TenantId:       loopTestTenant,
303			DeadlineUnixMs: req.GetDeadlineUnixMs(),
304			ProtocolMajor:  ProtocolMajor,
305			Attempt:        req.GetAttempt(),
306			Payload:        &strawpb.Envelope_AssignRequest{AssignRequest: &reqCopy},
307		}
308	
309		raw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(env)
310		if err != nil {
311			t.Fatalf("MarshalEnvelope() error = %v", err)
312		}
313	
314		var reply []byte
315	
316		deadline := time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second)
317		for {
318			msg, reqErr := h.controlConn.Request(subject, raw, 200*time.Millisecond)
319			if reqErr == nil {
320				reply = msg.Data
321	
322				break
323			}
324	
325			if time.Now().After(deadline) {
326				t.Fatalf("assign request %s: %v", requestID, reqErr)
327			}
328		}
329	
330		replyEnv, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(reply)
331		if err != nil {
332			t.Fatalf("UnmarshalEnvelope(reply) error = %v", err)
333		}
334	
335		return replyEnv.GetAssignAck()
336	}

90	// (docs/planning/29 "Worker Graceful Shutdown", steps 2-3).
91	func (w *Worker) Serve(ctx context.Context) error {
92		subject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID)
93		if err != nil {
94			return fmt.Errorf("assignment subject: %w", err)
95		}
96	
97		sub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(subject, w.handleAssign)
98		if err != nil {
99			return fmt.Errorf("subscribe assignment: %w", err)
100		}
101	
102		err = w.conn.Flush()
103		if err != nil {
104			_ = sub.Unsubscribe()
105	
106			return fmt.Errorf("flush assignment subscription: %w", err)
107		}
108	
109		<-ctx.Done()
110	
111		w.mu.Lock()
112		w.draining = true
113		w.mu.Unlock()
114	
115		_ = sub.Unsubscribe()
116	
117		drained := make(chan struct{})
118	
119		go func() {
120			w.wg.Wait()
121			close(drained)
122		}()
123	
124		select {
125		case <-drained:
126		case <-time.After(workerDrainTimeout):
127			w.abandonInFlight()
128			<-drained
129		}
130	
131		return nil
132	}
133	
134	func (w *Worker) abandonInFlight() {
135		w.mu.Lock()
136		cancels := make([]context.CancelFunc, 0, len(w.cancels))
137		for _, cancel := range w.cancels {
138			cancels = append(cancels, cancel)
139		}
140		w.mu.Unlock()
141	
142		for _, cancel := range cancels {
143			cancel()
144		}
145	}
146	
147	// handleAssign implements the executor side of the assignment flow
148	// (docs/planning/12 "Assignment Flow", steps 3-5): evaluate and reserve
149	// capacity, subscribe and flush the request-scoped c2e subject before
150	// accepting, then reply with AssignAck.
151	func (w *Worker) handleAssign(msg *nats.Msg) {
152		env, decodeErr := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(msg.Data)
153		if decodeErr != nil {
154			w.reply(msg, nil, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})

  "new_string": "// stop is a plain signal channel rather than a context.Context: the caller\n// decides exactly when draining begins (e.g. after sending a draining\n// heartbeat), which a context inherited from the caller's own lifecycle\n// cannot express without racing that ordering.\nfunc (w *Worker) Serve(stop <-chan struct{}) error {\n\tsubject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"assignment subject: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tsub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(subject, w.handleAssign)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"subscribe assignment: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\terr = w.conn.Flush()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = sub.Unsubscribe()\n\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"flush assignment subscription: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t<-stop\n\n\tw.mu.Lock()"

34	// Worker runs the live NATS assignment execution loop for one registered
35	// worker session (docs/planning/12 "Assignment Flow", docs/planning/09 steps
36	// 13-21).
37	type Worker struct {
38		conn      *natsx.Connection
39		id        Identity
40		executor  *Executor
41		sessionID string
42	
43		maxConcurrency uint32
44	
45		mu       sync.Mutex
46		active   uint32
47		draining bool
48		cancels  map[string]context.CancelFunc
49	
50		wg sync.WaitGroup
51	}
52	
53	// NewWorker builds a Worker bound to a live registered session.
54	func NewWorker(conn *natsx.Connection, id Identity, executor *Executor, sessionID string, maxConcurrency uint32) (*Worker, error) {
55		if conn == nil {
56			return nil, errConnRequired
57		}
58	
59		if executor == nil {
60			return nil, errExecutorRequired
61		}
62	
63		err := natsx.ValidateSubjectToken(sessionID)
64		if err != nil {
65			return nil, fmt.Errorf("session_id: %w", err)
66		}
67	
68		return &Worker{
69			conn:           conn,
70			id:             id,
71			executor:       executor,
72			sessionID:      sessionID,
73			maxConcurrency: maxConcurrency,
74			cancels:        make(map[string]context.CancelFunc),
75		}, nil
76	}
77	
78	// ActiveRequests reports the number of assignments currently executing.
79	func (w *Worker) ActiveRequests() uint32 {
80		w.mu.Lock()
81		defer w.mu.Unlock()
82	
83		return w.active
84	}
85	
86	// Serve subscribes to this worker's exact-session assignment subject (never a
87	// queue group, per docs/planning/12) and processes assignments until stop is
88	// closed. It then stops accepting new assignments and drains in-flight
89	// requests until they finish or workerDrainTimeout elapses (docs/planning/29
90	// "Worker Graceful Shutdown", steps 2-3). stop is a plain signal channel
91	// rather than a context.Context: the caller decides exactly when draining
92	// begins (e.g. after sending a draining heartbeat), which a context
93	// inherited from the caller's own lifecycle cannot express without racing
94	// that ordering.
95	func (w *Worker) Serve(stop <-chan struct{}) error {
96		subject, err := natsx.AssignmentSubject(w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID)
97		if err != nil {
98			return fmt.Errorf("assignment subject: %w", err)
99		}
100	
101		sub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(subject, w.handleAssign)
102		if err != nil {
103			return fmt.Errorf("subscribe assignment: %w", err)
104		}
105	
106		err = w.conn.Flush()
107		if err != nil {
108			_ = sub.Unsubscribe()
109	
110			return fmt.Errorf("flush assignment subscription: %w", err)
111		}
112	
113		<-stop
114	
115		w.mu.Lock()
116		w.draining = true
117		w.mu.Unlock()
118	
119		_ = sub.Unsubscribe()
120	
121		drained := make(chan struct{})
122	
123		go func() {
124			w.wg.Wait()
125			close(drained)
126		}()
127	
128		select {
129		case <-drained:
130		case <-time.After(workerDrainTimeout):
131			w.abandonInFlight()
132			<-drained
133		}
134	
135		return nil
136	}
137	
138	func (w *Worker) abandonInFlight() {
139		w.mu.Lock()
140		cancels := make([]context.CancelFunc, 0, len(w.cancels))
141		for _, cancel := range w.cancels {
142			cancels = append(cancels, cancel)
143		}
144		w.mu.Unlock()
145	
146		for _, cancel := range cancels {
147			cancel()
148		}
149	}
150	
151	// handleAssign implements the executor side of the assignment flow
152	// (docs/planning/12 "Assignment Flow", steps 3-5): evaluate and reserve
153	// capacity, subscribe and flush the request-scoped c2e subject before
154	// accepting, then reply with AssignAck.
155	func (w *Worker) handleAssign(msg *nats.Msg) {
156		env, decodeErr := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(msg.Data)
157		if decodeErr != nil {
158			w.reply(msg, nil, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})
159	
160			return
161		}
162	
163		req := env.GetAssignRequest()
164	
165		code := EvaluateAssignment(req, w.snapshotCapacity())
166		if code != strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED {
167			w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: code})
168	
169			return
170		}
171	
172		requestID := env.GetRequestId()
173	
174		c2eSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionControlToExecutor)
175		if err != nil {
176			w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})
177	
178			return
179		}
180	
181		e2cSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionExecutorToControl)
182		if err != nil {
183			w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})
184	
185			return
186		}
187	
188		frames := make(chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, streamFrameChannelBuffer)
189	
190		c2eSub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(c2eSubject, func(m *nats.Msg) {
191			frames <- decodeStreamFrame(m.Data)
192		})
193		if err != nil {
194			w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})
195	
196			return
197		}
198	
199		err = w.conn.Flush()
200		if err != nil {
201			_ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe()
202			w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})
203	
204			return
205		}
206	
207		reqCtx, cancel := requestContext(env.GetDeadlineUnixMs())
208		w.reserve(requestID, cancel)
209	
210		w.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED})
211	
212		w.wg.Add(1)
213	
214		go func() {
215			defer w.wg.Done()
216			defer func() { _ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe() }()
217			defer w.release(requestID)
218	
219			w.runRequest(reqCtx, cancel, req, env, frames, e2cSubject)
220		}()
221	}
222	
223	func (w *Worker) snapshotCapacity() Capacity {
224		w.mu.Lock()
225		defer w.mu.Unlock()
226	
227		return Capacity{
228			Draining:       w.draining,
229			ActiveRequests: w.active,
230			MaxConcurrency: w.maxConcurrency,
231			SupportedModes: p0SupportedModes,
232		}
233	}
234	
235	func (w *Worker) reserve(requestID string, cancel context.CancelFunc) {
236		w.mu.Lock()
237		defer w.mu.Unlock()
238	
239		w.active++
240		w.cancels[requestID] = cancel
241	}
242	
243	func (w *Worker) release(requestID string) {
244		w.mu.Lock()
245		defer w.mu.Unlock()
246	
247		w.active--
248		delete(w.cancels, requestID)
249	}
250	
251	func requestContext(deadlineUnixMs int64) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
252		if deadlineUnixMs <= 0 {
253			return context.WithCancel(context.Background())
254		}
255	
256		return context.WithDeadline(context.Background(), time.UnixMilli(deadlineUnixMs))
257	}
258	
259	// runRequest waits for RequestStart and its inline body over the c2e stream,
260	// executes the outbound request, and publishes the resulting frames to e2c
261	// (docs/planning/09 steps 16-21). If RequestStart never arrives (deadline,
262	// protocol error, or a pre-start Cancel), nothing is published: Control
263	// synthesizes the terminal outcome per docs/planning/09 "Terminal Rule".
264	func (w *Worker) runRequest(reqCtx context.Context, cancel context.CancelFunc, req *strawpb.AssignRequest, env *strawpb.Envelope, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, e2cSubject string) {
265		defer cancel()
266	
267		validator := natsx.NewStreamValidator(req.GetAttempt(), req.GetInitialUploadCreditBytes(), frameIdleTimeout, nil)
268	
269		start, body, ok := readRequestBody(reqCtx, validator, frames, req.GetExpectedUploadBytes())
270		if !ok {
271			return
272		}
273	
274		resultCh := make(chan []*strawpb.StreamFrame, 1)
275	
276		go func() {
277			resultCh <- w.executor.Execute(reqCtx, start, body, req.GetAttempt())
278		}()
279	
280		result, canceled, reason := waitForResult(resultCh, frames, validator, cancel)
281		if canceled {
282			result = applyCancellation(result, reason)
283		}
284	
285		w.publish(e2cSubject, env, result)
286	}
287	
288	// readRequestBody accepts c2e frames until RequestStart and its full inline
289	// body (sized by AssignRequest.ExpectedUploadBytes) have arrived. P0 does not
290	// support BodyRef (docs/planning/16), so the body is always inline DataFrames.
291	func readRequestBody(ctx context.Context, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, expectedUploadBytes int64) (*strawpb.RequestStart, []byte, bool) {
292		var (
293			start    *strawpb.RequestStart
294			body     []byte
295			received uint64
296		)
297	
298		var expected uint64
299		if expectedUploadBytes > 0 {
300			expected = uint64(expectedUploadBytes)
301		}
302	
303		ticker := time.NewTicker(idleCheckInterval)
304		defer ticker.Stop()
305	
306		for start == nil || received < expected {
307			select {
308			case <-ctx.Done():
309				return nil, nil, false
310			case frame, ok := <-frames:
311				if !ok {
312					return nil, nil, false
313				}
314	
315				outcome := validator.Accept(frame)
316				if outcome == natsx.FrameDuplicate {
317					continue
318				}
319	
320				if outcome != natsx.FrameAccepted {
321					return nil, nil, false
322				}
323	
324				switch p := frame.GetPayload().(type) {
325				case *strawpb.StreamFrame_RequestStart:
326					start = p.RequestStart
327				case *strawpb.StreamFrame_Data:
328					body = append(body, p.Data.GetData()...)
329					received += uint64(len(p.Data.GetData()))
330				case *strawpb.StreamFrame_Credit:
331					// Download credit grant for e2c response bytes. P0 executes
332					// the whole response in one pass (Executor.Execute) rather
333					// than chunking by credit; response buffering per credit is
334					// Control's responsibility (docs/tasks/p0/24).
335				default:
336					return nil, nil, false
337				}
338			case <-ticker.C:
339				if validator.IdleExpired() {
340					return nil, nil, false
341				}
342			}
343		}
344	
345		return start, body, true
346	}
347	
348	// waitForResult waits for the outbound execution to finish while still
349	// accepting c2e frames, most importantly a CancelFrame, which cancels
350	// execCtx so Executor.Execute aborts promptly.
351	func waitForResult(resultCh <-chan []*strawpb.StreamFrame, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, cancel context.CancelFunc) ([]*strawpb.StreamFrame, bool, string) {
352		for {
353			select {
354			case result := <-resultCh:
355				return result, false, ""
356			case frame, ok := <-frames:
357				if !ok {
358					continue
359				}
360	
361				if validator.Accept(frame) != natsx.FrameAccepted {
362					continue
363				}
364	
365				cancelFrame, isCancel := frame.GetPayload().(*strawpb.StreamFrame_Cancel)
366				if !isCancel {
367					continue
368				}
369	
370				cancel()
371	
372				return <-resultCh, true, cancelFrame.Cancel.GetReason()
373			}
374		}
375	}
376	
377	// applyCancellation replaces the terminal frame of a Cancel-aborted execution
378	// with a CancelledFrame (docs/planning/09 "Terminal Rule"), unless execution
379	// had already completed successfully before the cancel took effect
380	// (cancellation is best effort).
381	func applyCancellation(frames []*strawpb.StreamFrame, reason string) []*strawpb.StreamFrame {
382		if len(frames) == 0 {
383			return frames
384		}
385	
386		last := frames[len(frames)-1]
387		if _, ok := last.GetPayload().(*strawpb.StreamFrame_End); ok {
388			return frames
389		}
390	
391		frames[len(frames)-1] = &strawpb.StreamFrame{
392			StreamSeq: last.GetStreamSeq(),
393			Attempt:   last.GetAttempt(),
394			Payload:   &strawpb.StreamFrame_Cancelled{Cancelled: &strawpb.CancelledFrame{Reason: reason}},
395		}
396	
397		return frames
398	}
399	
400	func (w *Worker) publish(subject string, env *strawpb.Envelope, frames []*strawpb.StreamFrame) {
401		for _, frame := range frames {
402			out := &strawpb.Envelope{
403				RequestId:      env.GetRequestId(),
404				TenantId:       env.GetTenantId(),
405				TraceId:        env.GetTraceId(),
406				DeadlineUnixMs: env.GetDeadlineUnixMs(),
407				ProtocolMajor:  ProtocolMajor,
408				Attempt:        env.GetAttempt(),
409				Payload:        &strawpb.Envelope_StreamFrame{StreamFrame: frame},
410			}
411	
412			raw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(out)
413			if err != nil {
414				return
415			}
416	
417			err = w.conn.Publish(subject, raw)
418			if err != nil {
419				return
420			}
421		}
422	}
423	
424	func decodeStreamFrame(raw []byte) *strawpb.StreamFrame {
425		env, err := natsx.UnmarshalEnvelope(raw)
426		if err != nil {
427			return nil
428		}
429	
430		return env.GetStreamFrame()
431	}
432	
433	func (w *Worker) reply(msg *nats.Msg, env *strawpb.Envelope, ack *strawpb.AssignAck) {
434		reply := &strawpb.Envelope{
435			ProtocolMajor: ProtocolMajor,
436			Payload:       &strawpb.Envelope_AssignAck{AssignAck: ack},
437		}
438	
439		if env != nil {
440			reply.RequestId = env.GetRequestId()
441			reply.TenantId = env.GetTenantId()
442			reply.TraceId = env.GetTraceId()
443			reply.DeadlineUnixMs = env.GetDeadlineUnixMs()
444			reply.Attempt = env.GetAttempt()
445		}
446	
447		raw, err := natsx.MarshalEnvelope(reply)
448		if err != nil {
449			return
450		}
451	
452		_ = msg.Respond(raw)
453	}
454

  "old_string": "\trequestID := env.GetRequestId()\n\n\tc2eSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionControlToExecutor)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\te2cSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionExecutorToControl)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tframes := make(chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, streamFrameChannelBuffer)\n\n\tc2eSub, err := w.conn.Subscribe(c2eSubject, func(m *nats.Msg) {\n\t\tframes <- decodeStreamFrame(m.Data)\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\terr = w.conn.Flush()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe()\n\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\treqCtx, cancel := requestContext(env.GetDeadlineUnixMs())\n\tw.reserve(requestID, cancel)\n\n\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED})\n\n\tw.wg.Add(1)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer w.wg.Done()\n\t\tdefer func() { _ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe() }()\n\t\tdefer w.release(requestID)\n\n\t\tw.runRequest(reqCtx, cancel, req, env, frames, e2cSubject)\n\t}()\n}",
  "new_string": "\trequestID := env.GetRequestId()\n\n\tc2eSubject, e2cSubject, c2eSub, frames, ok := w.prepareRequestStream(env)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_REJECTED_ERROR})\n\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t_ = c2eSubject\n\n\treqCtx, cancel := requestContext(env.GetDeadlineUnixMs())\n\tw.reserve(requestID, cancel)\n\n\tw.reply(msg, env, &strawpb.AssignAck{Code: strawpb.AssignAckCode_ASSIGN_ACK_ACCEPTED})\n\n\tw.wg.Go(func() {\n\t\tdefer func() { _ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe() }()\n\t\tdefer w.release(requestID)\n\n\t\tw.runRequest(reqCtx, cancel, req, env, frames, e2cSubject)\n\t})\n}\n\n// prepareRequestStream resolves the request-scoped c2e/e2c subjects and\n// subscribes and flushes c2e (docs/planning/12 \"Assignment Flow\" step 4)\n// before the caller replies with AssignAck.\nfunc (w *Worker) prepareRequestStream(env *strawpb.Envelope) (c2eSubject, e2cSubject string, c2eSub *nats.Subscription, frames chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, ok bool) {\n\trequestID := env.GetRequestId()\n\n\tc2eSubject, err := natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionControlToExecutor)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\n\te2cSubject, err = natsx.StreamSubject(requestID, w.id.WorkerID, w.sessionID, natsx.DirectionExecutorToControl)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\n\tframes = make(chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, streamFrameChannelBuffer)\n\n\tc2eSub, err = w.conn.Subscribe(c2eSubject, func(m *nats.Msg) {\n\t\tframes <- decodeStreamFrame(m.Data)\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\n\terr = w.conn.Flush()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = c2eSub.Unsubscribe()\n\n\t\treturn \"\", \"\", nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\n\treturn c2eSubject, e2cSubject, c2eSub, frames, true\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// readRequestBody accepts c2e frames until RequestStart and its full inline\n// body (sized by AssignRequest.ExpectedUploadBytes) have arrived. P0 does not\n// support BodyRef (docs/planning/16), so the body is always inline DataFrames.\nfunc readRequestBody(ctx context.Context, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, expectedUploadBytes int64) (*strawpb.RequestStart, []byte, bool) {\n\tvar (\n\t\tstart    *strawpb.RequestStart\n\t\tbody     []byte\n\t\treceived uint64\n\t)\n\n\tvar expected uint64\n\tif expectedUploadBytes > 0 {\n\t\texpected = uint64(expectedUploadBytes)\n\t}\n\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(idleCheckInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor start == nil || received < expected {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\tcase frame, ok := <-frames:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\toutcome := validator.Accept(frame)\n\t\t\tif outcome == natsx.FrameDuplicate {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif outcome != natsx.FrameAccepted {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tswitch p := frame.GetPayload().(type) {\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_RequestStart:\n\t\t\t\tstart = p.RequestStart\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Data:\n\t\t\t\tbody = append(body, p.Data.GetData()...)\n\t\t\t\treceived += uint64(len(p.Data.GetData()))\n\t\t\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Credit:\n\t\t\t\t// Download credit grant for e2c response bytes. P0 executes\n\t\t\t\t// the whole response in one pass (Executor.Execute) rather\n\t\t\t\t// than chunking by credit; response buffering per credit is\n\t\t\t\t// Control's responsibility (docs/tasks/p0/24).\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tif validator.IdleExpired() {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn start, body, true\n}",
  "new_string": "// readRequestBody accepts c2e frames until RequestStart and its full inline\n// body (sized by AssignRequest.ExpectedUploadBytes) have arrived. P0 does not\n// support BodyRef (docs/planning/16), so the body is always inline DataFrames.\nfunc readRequestBody(ctx context.Context, validator *natsx.StreamValidator, frames <-chan *strawpb.StreamFrame, expectedUploadBytes int64) (*strawpb.RequestStart, []byte, bool) {\n\tstate := &requestBodyState{}\n\tif expectedUploadBytes > 0 {\n\t\tstate.expected = uint64(expectedUploadBytes)\n\t}\n\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(idleCheckInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor !state.complete() {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\tcase frame, ok := <-frames:\n\t\t\tif !ok || !state.accept(validator, frame) {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tif validator.IdleExpired() {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn state.start, state.body, true\n}\n\n// requestBodyState accumulates c2e RequestStart/DataFrame payloads into the\n// inline request body Executor.Execute expects.\ntype requestBodyState struct {\n\tstart    *strawpb.RequestStart\n\tbody     []byte\n\treceived uint64\n\texpected uint64\n}\n\nfunc (s *requestBodyState) complete() bool {\n\treturn s.start != nil && s.received >= s.expected\n}\n\n// accept applies one c2e frame to the body state. It returns false when the\n// frame is a protocol violation, an unexpected payload, or a pre-start\n// Cancel, any of which abort the request per docs/planning/09 \"Terminal\n// Rule\" (nothing is published; Control synthesizes the outcome).\nfunc (s *requestBodyState) accept(validator *natsx.StreamValidator, frame *strawpb.StreamFrame) bool {\n\toutcome := validator.Accept(frame)\n\tif outcome == natsx.FrameDuplicate {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\n\tif outcome != natsx.FrameAccepted {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tswitch p := frame.GetPayload().(type) {\n\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_RequestStart:\n\t\ts.start = p.RequestStart\n\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Data:\n\t\ts.body = append(s.body, p.Data.GetData()...)\n\t\ts.received += uint64(len(p.Data.GetData()))\n\tcase *strawpb.StreamFrame_Credit:\n\t\t// Download credit grant for e2c response bytes. P0 executes the\n\t\t// whole response in one pass (Executor.Execute) rather than\n\t\t// chunking by credit; response buffering per credit is Control's\n\t\t// responsibility (docs/tasks/p0/24).\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\treturn true\n}"
}
